### S0.7 — registration API + pool claim (code-read of `../frontend-legacy` at `c88b8fefb` on `kh-zcp-pool-exhausted-fallback` and the public spec `../zcp-openapi-2026-08-24.yml`; one non-mutating live probe; no account created)

| Fact                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | Where / how                                                                                                                                                               |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Registration is `POST /registration`**, body `RequestUserRegistration`: required `email`, `languageId`, `accountName`, `name`; optional `password` (required unless passkey), `token` (Cloudflare Turnstile), `passkey`, `promoCode`, `reference`. The GUI additionally sends **`claimZcpPool: true`** (only when the route carries `?zcp=true`) and `deviceName` — both absent from the spec, accepted by the backend. Response `ResponseAuthRegistration` = `{auth:{accessToken, refreshToken, expiresIn, twoFAMethods, sudoUntil}, user, webAuthn}` plus undocumented **`zcpClaimed?: boolean`** — absent or `true` = a pool project was claimed (or the signup was not pool-aware), `false` = pool exhausted. The session token comes back immediately.                                                              | spec L6682-6716 / L6957-6970; `registration.model.ts:8-18`, `registration.page.ts:109-111`, `libs/zef/src/auth/auth.model.ts:63-71`                                       |
| **No e-mail-verification gate before login**: `GET /user/verify-email/{id}` finalises the mailed token and `PUT /user/{id}/send-verification-email` re-sends, but nothing blocks `/auth/login` on an unverified address. **TOTP is optional and post-signup only** (`/2fa/totp/registration/start`+`/finish` under a Bearer session; `/2fa/totp/login` only once enabled). No terms/consent field exists on the request.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | spec L20296, L20008, L9975; GUI registration module                                                                                                                       |
| **The captcha is not a required field at the API's validation layer.** Live: `POST /registration` with body `{}` → `400` `invalidUserInput`, metadata names only `accountName`, `email`, `languageId`, `name` as `field is required` — no `token` entry. Response carries `access-control-allow-origin: *`. Whether a missing/invalid Turnstile token is rejected later (score / rate-limit) is NOT determined — see `questions.md` Q-10.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | `curl -sS -i -X POST $BASE/registration -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{}'`, 2026-08-28                                                                          |
| The Turnstile site key the GUI uses is `environment.turnstileToken` (`0x4AAAAAABkfI4SNvJav8428` in `environment.local.ts:33`); whether Cloudflare binds it to the GUI's hostnames is not visible from any repo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `registration-form.component.ts:6,17,25`                                                                                                                                  |
| **After `zcpClaimed` the GUI never polls REST**: it waits for the control-plane stack in a WebSocket-pushed service-stack subscription (`listSubscribe`), 30 s timeout, then for the `stack.build` process and subdomain settlement over the same push streams.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `service-stack-base.effect.ts:158`, `zcp-pool-claim-base.effect.ts:226-283` (`#resolveAndEnterSelection$`), `#awaitZcpBuildOutcome$`, `#awaitWorkspaceReachable$`         |
| **Pool-exhausted fallback = two REST calls**: `POST /client/{clientId}/project` (`RequestPostProject`) when the account has no project, else the first project is reused; then **`PUT /project/{id}/first-class-recipe/development-container`** with `{serviceImportYaml, recipeSource: "zeropsio/zcp", createIntegrationToken: true}` (`RequestFirstClassRecipeDevelopmentContainer`, required `serviceImportYaml`). A dedicated first-class-recipe route, not a generic `service-stack/import`. The exact `serviceImportYaml` the GUI generates (`ZeropsYamlBuilder.generate()`) was not traced — needed before S4 runs this for real.                                                                                                                                                                                   | spec L13030-13040, L5446-5463; `zcp-pool-claim-base.effect.ts:431-476`, `:704-753`, `:661-668`; `service-stack-base.api.ts:161-178`; `control-plane-dialog.constant.ts:7` |
| **GitHub OAuth signup**: `GET /github/auth-url?action=REGISTRATION&redirectUrl=<fixed callback>&nonHaRecipe=&haRecipe=&email=` → callback page parses `code`+`state` → `POST /github/user-registration {code, state, email?}` → a `ResponseAuthLogin` shape that may carry `zcpClaimed`. **The pool claim on this path is unimplemented**: `github-auth.page.ts:100-111` says the claim rides in the OAuth `state` as `zcp:true`, but no producer attaches it anywhere (`external-auth-buttons.container.ts:57-67` → `github.effect.ts:35-48` → `github.service.ts:23-37` pass only `authMode`/recipe ids; repo-wide grep finds no other call site). The callback URL is a fixed value registered on Zerops' own GitHub OAuth App — a third-party origin cannot add its own redirect without a platform-side registration. | spec L5521-5532; the cited GUI files                                                                                                                                      |

### S0.12 — envelope extraction from the provider stream (live `claude -p` on `z3-eval`'s zcp + code read of `../zcp` and the fork; Codex code-read only, not logged in)

| Fact                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | How it was established                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **The `StateEnvelope` never crosses the wire.** `zerops_workflow action="status"` (the only lifecycle-status carrier) computes the envelope and returns `textResult(RenderStatus(…))` — markdown prose (`## Status / Phase: … / Services: … / Guidance: … / Next:`); the handler's structured-output return (`any`, the Go MCP SDK's `structuredContent` slot) is `nil`, and `structuredContent` appears nowhere in `internal/`. `workflow.Response{Envelope…}` (`json:"envelope"`) is consumed only by `RenderStatus`. The brief's §3 line "StateEnvelope: JSON attached to every workflow-aware tool result" describes an intent, not the code.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `../zcp/internal/tools/workflow.go:906-941`, `internal/tools/workflow_develop.go:271-294`, `internal/workflow/render.go:15-26`, `internal/tools/convert.go:106-120`; `grep -rni structuredcontent internal/` → 0 hits (zcp `64f63ae8`) |
| Live, Claude Code stream-json: a `status` call's `tool_result` is one `{type:"text", text}` block of **8 839 bytes of markdown** (804 bytes of state header, the rest atom guidance); `jq -e .` fails on it, zero occurrences of `envelope`. `zerops_discover`'s result IS verbatim JSON (1 592 bytes, parses; keys `project`, `services[]`, `warnings[]` — a narrower shape, no phase/workSession/closeMode). Tool results arrive as arrays of text blocks, never bare strings; no truncation or double escaping. Whole turn: 8–9 events, 12.9 KB (discover) / 26.8 KB (status).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `claude -p '…zerops_workflow action="status"…' --output-format stream-json --verbose --allowedTools mcp__zerops__zerops_workflow` from `/var/www` on the container; `jq` over the jsonl, 2026-08-28                                    |
| The two `launch`/`export` "envelopes" in `internal/tools/launch_status_recovery.go:18` and `workflow_export.go` are bespoke JSON shapes returned via `jsonResult`, not `workflow.StateEnvelope`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | code read                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| **Fork seams — MCP tool results reach one provider-agnostic bus unmodified.** Claude: `ClaudeAdapter.ts:1727` runs the CLI through `@anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk` `query()` with `settingSources: ["user","project","local"]` (`:1243-1247`, why the container's `~/.claude.json` `mcpServers.zerops` is picked up); `handleUserMessage` (`:2740-2830`) emits `item.completed` with `payload.data = {toolName, input, result: <raw tool_result block>}` — no cap, no stringify. Codex: the driver spawns the persistent `codex app-server` (`CodexSessionRuntime.ts:1185-1204`, JSON-RPC via `effect-codex-app-server`), not `codex exec --json`; `mapItemLifecycle` (`CodexAdapter.ts:466-501`) emits `item.completed` with the raw `V2ItemCompletedNotification` whose `mcpToolCall` item is `{server, tool, arguments, result?: {content: unknown[], structuredContent?, _meta?}, error?, status}` (`schema.gen.ts:20437-20445`). Both map to `itemType: "mcp_tool_call"` (Claude by the `mcp__` name substring, `ClaudeAdapter.ts:736-771`; Codex natively, `CodexAdapter.ts:221-238`). Single hook for a reducer: `ProviderService.ts:234` `runtimeEventPubSub` / `publishRuntimeEvent` (`:286-293`). | `../z3/apps/server/src/provider/Layers/{ClaudeAdapter,CodexAdapter,CodexSessionRuntime,ProviderService}.ts`, `packages/effect-codex-app-server/src/_generated/schema.gen.ts` at `0e517b5b8`                                            |
| Codex CLI in the container: `codex-cli 0.150.1`; `codex exec --json` prints JSONL events, `-o/--output-last-message <file>` the final message — relevant only for headless scripts, not for the fork (which uses app-server). Not logged in (`~/.codex/auth.json` absent), no live run.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `codex --version`, `codex exec --help` on the container                                                                                                                                                                                |

### S0.11 — `zcp studio watch` / `topology` (live on `z3-eval`'s zcp, dev build `64f63ae8`, throwaway `s0watch1` = `valkey:single@7.2` NON_HA)

| Fact                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | How it was established                                                                                                                                     |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **`zcp studio watch` is a doorbell, not a data feed.** `Event` is `{"type": …}` with exactly three values `connected`, `topology-changed`, `disconnected` — no id, hostname, status or process field. It subscribes over the platform websocket (`POST /web-socket/login` → `wss://<host>/api/rest/public/web-socket/<receiverId>/<token>` → `POST /service-stack/search` with `subscriptionName=ServiceStack__list-subscription`, `wsOutputType: listStream`) and fires on any matching `type:"search"` push without parsing its `add`/`delete` payload. It never re-reads topology itself.                                                              | `../zcp/internal/dataconsole/watch/watch.go:1-30, 53-67, 188-211, 223-284`; live raw events `{"type":"connected"}` then `{"type":"topology-changed"}`      |
| **It fires only on list-membership changes.** Import of `s0watch1` → `topology-changed` at **+0.55 s** after the `zcli` call started (before zcli returned at +16.2 s); delete → `topology-changed` at **+10.0 s** (when the row vanished). `zcli service stop` + `start` on the same service (ACTIVE → STOPPING → … → ACTIVE, ~80 s) produced **zero** events. Foreign add/delete events from other subagents' services arrived the same way — unattributable from the event itself.                                                                                                                                                                     | `(sleep 1800 \| zcp studio watch)` from `/var/www` on the container with per-line `date +%s.%N`; zcli import/stop/start/delete with timestamps, 2026-08-28 |
| **`zcp studio topology` is a direct read (no ES lag)**: `ops.Discover` → `client.ListServicesDirect` (`GET /project/{id}/service-stack`) → `FillSubdomainURLs` → `EnrichWithMetaStatus`. Polled every 1 s it showed `s0watch1` `CREATING` at **+1.05 s**, `ACTIVE` at **+16.4 s** (valkey, no build), `STOPPING` at +1.7 s after stop, `DELETING` at +3.0 s and gone at +9.4 s after delete. One call = 0.26 s real for 5 services. Per service it carries `hostname`, `serviceId`, `type`, `status`, `adoptionState`, `isInfrastructure`, `subdomainEnabled`/`subdomainUrl` — no live process (build/deploy) state; that is `ops.ProjectActivity`'s job. | `../zcp/cmd/zcp/studio.go:123-149`, `internal/ops/discover.go:164`; the 1 s poll loop on the container                                                     |

On this container `ZCP_API_KEY` + `ZEROPS_ClientId` sit in the process environment (there is no `/var/www/.mcp.json`); `auth.ResolveCredentials()` reads env first.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | `/tmp/s0watch.err`; `../zcp/internal/auth/auth.go:121`                                                                                                     |

### S0.3 / S0.4 / S0.13 — git over the sshfs mount vs `ssh <host> git -C /var/www` (live on `z3-eval`: three throwaway `nodejs@22` dev services `s0git1..3`, `facebook/docusaurus` at `4cbfcfa1` depth-1 — 4 246 tracked files, `.git` 39 MB; `s0git1` additionally with a `pnpm` `node_modules` of 76 684 files, 82 716 files total; mounted on zcp with the product's exact `sshfs` primitive; all deleted after)

Versions: hosts git 2.34.1 (3 vCPU, 1280 MB); zcp git 2.43.0, sshfs 3.7.3 / FUSE 3.14.0. `/proc/loadavg` inside these containers reports the PHYSICAL NODE's load (14–28 with no busy process) — use `ps --sort=-pcpu` to judge idleness. Headline cells re-run at the end reproduced within 10–20 %. Raw scripts + logs: session scratch `s03/`.

| Fact                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | How it was established                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **One T3 turn is 22 git invocations, not 12–20**: `captureCheckpoint` (`GitVcsDriver.ts:710-790`) = 7 (`rev-parse --git-common-dir`, `rev-parse --verify HEAD`, `read-tree HEAD`, `add -A -- .`, `write-tree`, `commit-tree`, `update-ref`), all but the first/last under a fresh `GIT_INDEX_FILE=<gitCommonDir>/t3-checkpoint-index-<uuid>` (it does not copy the real index — `read-tree HEAD` into a fresh one), run TWICE per turn (baseline + completion, `CheckpointReactor.ts:355, 509-526, 668-685`) each preceded by `rev-parse --verify --quiet <ref>^{commit}`; the status block (`GitVcsDriverCore.ts:1584`) = `status --porcelain=2 --branch` + `diff HEAD --numstat --` + `rev-parse … @{upstream}`; review = `ls-files --others --exclude-standard -z` + range diffs + one `diff --no-index` PER untracked file. Poller `DEFAULT_VCS_STATUS_REFRESH_INTERVAL` = 30 s per open cwd. | code read of the fork at `0e517b5b8`; the replicated sequence is `bench.sh`                                                            |
| **Full turn, clean tree (median / max ms)**: mount **12 699 / 13 170** · ssh no-mux 2 601 / 2 738 · **ssh + ControlMaster 1 370 / 1 916** · on the host 1 037 / 1 429. Checkpoint capture alone: mount 5 494 · no-mux 963 · mux 601 · host 452. `status --porcelain=2 --branch` back-to-back: mount 248 (cold 6 693) · no-mux 121 · mux 85 · host 62. One `rev-parse --show-toplevel`: 5 / 61 / 19 / 2. Re-run later: mount 13 534, mux 1 253, host 1 373. **The mount is 9× a multiplexed ssh and the ssh runner is 1.3× the host floor.**                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | 3 runs per cell, `date +%s%N` around each sequence, `GIT_INDEX_FILE` in the repo's `.git` (M1) / the host (M3, M4), 2026-08-28         |
| **The cost is the tree walk, not the change count**: 20 edited tracked files + 50 new untracked files written through the mount changed nothing — checkpoint mount 5 955 · mux 621 · host 445; status 262 / 74 / 65.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | same cells after the dirtying writes                                                                                                   |
| **Status at the real poll cadence is 1.4–2.2 s over the mount**, not 248 ms: the 30 s poller always outlives the 20 s `dcache_timeout`, so every production poll is the cold case (measured with 30 s gaps: 5 451, 1 419, 2 134, 2 159 ms).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | `status --porcelain=2 --branch` on M1 with `sleep 30` between runs                                                                     |
| **The workspace rescan is a native `FileFinder` scan with a 15 s timeout** (`WorkspaceSearchIndex.ts`, `WORKSPACE_INDEX_SCAN_TIMEOUT_MS = 15_000`, `WORKSPACE_INDEX_MAX_ENTRIES = 25_000`; `WorkspaceEntries.ts` is only the per-keystroke single-level browse). A full recursive readdir of 94 629 dirents over the mount took 14 112, 6 056, 16 015, 2 619 ms across runs (the 20 s dir cache decides each) — twice past the timeout (→ `WorkspaceSearchIndexScanTimedOut`, index invalidated); on the host 409 / 489 ms; `ssh host find` 483 / 493 ms. Skipping `node_modules`: mount 375 (cold 1 379), host 41.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | `rescan.js` (node recursive `fs.readdir`) on zcp over the mount vs on the host; `find . -not -path './.git/*' -print` piped to a count |
| **ssh handshake: 59 ms unmultiplexed vs 8 ms multiplexed** (`ssh host true`, 20-iteration loops; a multiplexed `git rev-parse` round trip 19 ms). At 24 round trips per turn the master saves ~1.2 s per repo per turn.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | `-F /dev/null -o ControlPath=none -o ControlMaster=no` vs the container default                                                        |
| **zcp already ships ControlMaster for every host**: the managed `# ZCP:BEGIN … # ZCP:END` block in the container's `~/.ssh/config` sets `Host *` → `User zerops`, `StrictHostKeyChecking no`, `UserKnownHostsFile /dev/null`, `LogLevel ERROR`, `ControlMaster auto`, `ControlPath /tmp/ssh-mux-%r@%h:%p`, `ControlPersist 600`. A first "no-mux" run silently multiplexed until forced off with `-F /dev/null`. A driver should still pin the options itself, not inherit them.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | `cat ~/.ssh/config` on the container; template `../zcp/internal/content/templates/ssh-config`                                          |
| **S0.4 forward — writes through the mount are write-through**: a new file, an edit of a tracked file, a delete, each made on zcp through the mount, were visible to host-side `git status` / `git diff --stat` at poll #0 of a 50 ms loop in all 3 trials (82–113 ms = the cost of `git status` over 82 k files, not a lag).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `vis_fwd.sh`                                                                                                                           |
| **S0.4 reverse — host-side changes reach the mount's file content and path lookups at once, but directory listings and stale entries lag exactly `dcache_timeout` = 20 s**: after a host-side create, `stat <newpath>` on zcp 11–23 ms but `ls <parent>` shows it only after 20 926–21 034 ms; a host-side edit / `git restore` shows new content in 14–33 ms; after a host-side delete, `stat` keeps succeeding for 20 836–20 949 ms. The product mount sets no cache options, so sshfs 3.7.3 defaults hold (`dir_cache=yes`, `dcache_timeout=20`, `dcache_max_size=10000`, `entry_timeout=1.0`, `attr_timeout=1.0`, `negative_timeout=0`, no `kernel_cache`, no `auto_cache`); `/proc/mounts` shows only `rw,nosuid,nodev,relatime,user_id=2023,group_id=2023`.                                                                                                                                 | `vis_rev.sh`, 100 ms polls, parent listing pre-warmed; `sshfs -h` for the defaults                                                     |
| **Fixing the reverse lag with mount options costs 3–27× on status**: a throwaway mount with `dir_cache=no,attr_timeout=0,entry_timeout=0` removed the lag (every reverse operation at poll #0) but `status --porcelain=2 --branch` over it = 6 620 ms median, cold 27 113 ms. Do not weaken the product mount; refresh host-side state over SSH instead.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | `/home/zerops/s0mnt-nocache` (direct `sshfs`, `fusermount -u` after)                                                                   |
| **inotify over the mount never fires for host-side changes**: `fs.watch` on zcp (repo dir + a file) saw nothing in 10 s for a host-side append/create/delete; the same append made on zcp through the mount fired `change` at the moment of the write. Any watcher must poll over SSH.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `watch.js`                                                                                                                             |
| **S0.13 — three repos per turn (checkpoint + status each)**: mount sequential **21 019** ms · ssh+mux sequential 2 333 · **ssh+mux parallel 802** (per repo 600 / 1 105 / 752). Repos without `node_modules` cost the same as the one with (the `.gitignore` short-circuits the walk; the TRACKED count drives it). Fan-out across hosts is near-linear: three repos ≈ one.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | `multi.sh`, one ControlMaster per host, `&`/`wait` for the parallel cell                                                               |
| **`add -A` without a `.gitignore` is the 4-minute case.** zcp's host-side init (`ops.GitEnsureRepoHeadCommand`, `internal/ops/git_identity.go:78-81`) writes NO `.gitignore` and leaves an empty-tree HEAD. With docusaurus's `.gitignore` removed (76 684 untracked `node_modules` files): checkpoint on the host 9 846 ms, over ssh 9 444 ms, over the mount **245 536 ms**; `status` stays 63–100 ms on every method because it collapses untracked directories — it never reveals the problem, only the checkpoint pays.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | same bench with `.gitignore` removed on `s0git1`                                                                                       |

### S0.5 — base path `/z3/` under nginx on the 8080 origin (live on `z3probe`, nginx.conf hand-edited + `nginx -s reload`, 2026-08-28 13:35–13:42 UTC)

| Fact                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | How it was established                                                                                                   |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| **The whole z3 chain works through a path prefix on 8080, cookie-less**: `location /z3/ { proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:3773/; proxy_http_version 1.1; Upgrade/Connection headers; proxy_read_timeout 86400s; }` placed beside `/healthz` outside the `__zcp_auth` gate → mint → `POST /z3/oauth/token` (form) `200` (same five scopes, `expires_in` 2 591 999) → `POST /z3/api/auth/websocket-ticket` `200` → `GET /z3/ws?wsTicket=…` **`101`** with a valid `Sec-WebSocket-Accept`; Node's `WebSocket` to `wss://…-8080…/z3/ws` opened in 43 ms and stayed open 60 s (native 3773 control: 27 ms, identical). `GET /` still `302 /zcp-login`; with the cookie, `/z3/` still reaches z3 (longest prefix beats `location /`). | `curl` chain from the laptop, no VPN; a Node script holding the socket                                                   |
| **Vite `base` is mandatory, not cosmetic**: `/z3/` serves the SPA index (19 884 B) whose refs are root-absolute (`<script src="/assets/index-….js">`, `<link href="/assets/index-….css">`): `/z3/assets/<x>` → `200`, `/assets/<x>` → `302 /zcp-login`. The page loads and every asset dies.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | `curl -s …/z3/` + the two asset URLs                                                                                     |
| **A wrong base path fails silently, never as a 404**: with `proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:3773` (no trailing slash, prefix kept) z3's SPA catch-all answers `200 text/html` for `/z3/.well-known/t3/environment` and `/z3/assets/*` alike — same on the native origin for any unknown prefix. A base-path bug looks like "the app loads but nothing works".                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | one curl each, then restored                                                                                             |
| **No collision with code-server 4.134.0**: its top-level routes are `/`, `/_static`, `/healthz`, `/login`, `/logout`, `/update`, `/proxy/:port`, `/absproxy/:port`, `/robots.txt` — nothing under `/z3/`. Two side facts: nginx's exact `location = /healthz` already shadows code-server's own `/healthz`; and **`/proxy/3773/…` (behind the cookie gate) already reaches z3** (`/proxy/3773/.well-known/t3/environment` → z3's JSON) — a second door that exists today.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | code-server's `src/node/routes/index.*` in the container; `curl -b __zcp_auth=… …/proxy/3773/.well-known/t3/environment` |
| **`z3probe`'s recipe never runs `install.sh`**: its `initCommands` are `cp /var/www/zcp-linux-amd64 /usr/local/bin/zcp`, `zcp init`, `sudo -E zcp init nginx` — a restart re-installs the DEPLOYED dev binary. "Restart = upgrade" (S0.2's `install.sh` half) is therefore measured on `z3-eval`, whose recipe is the platform's (S0.8 row), at the cost of one re-push (brief §4a).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | `printenv ZEROPS_YAML` on `z3probe`                                                                                      |

### S0.2 + S0.10 (restart half) — API restart of `z3probe` (2026-08-28 13:42:48 UTC, `PUT /service-stack/{id}/restart` with the owner's token → process `f9S43XpfS36CyshS2R9qgg` `stack.restart`)

| Fact                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | How it was established                                                                                                                     |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| **A restart is 19 s to z3 up, 17 s to `/healthz` answering, ~14 s of L7 `502`** ("Check if your application is running on a correct port"): call +0 s → last `200` −3 s → first `502` +3 s → kernel boot +8 s → binary re-copied +10 s → `zcp init nginx` / `initAt` +13 s → `zerops@z3` started +15 s → `/healthz 200` `z3Up:false` +17 s → `z3Up:true` +19 s; the `stack.restart` process `FINISHED` at +17.3 s. **The container is kept, not re-created** (`GET /service-stack/{id}/container` → same id `xjZMiUbpTIGA1VBIDwbrRQ`, `z3probe-runtime-4-376`, created 2026-08-27). | `curl -m 5 …/healthz` every 2 s from the laptop; `uptime -s`, binary mtime, `initAt`, `systemctl show zerops@z3` after; the process object |
| **A `zsc unit create`d unit survives a restart** — `zerops@s0probe.service` (`sleep infinity`) came back `active`, `UnitFileState=enabled`, `FragmentPath=/usr/lib/systemd/system/zerops@s0probe.service`. Former `questions.md` Q-01, now answered: anything registered at runtime through `zsc unit create` returns on its own.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | created before the restart, inspected after                                                                                                |
| **State survives a restart**: `/home/zerops/.t3` (with `state.sqlite` — 3 threads / 6 messages / 3 turns / 1 project / 81 orchestration events, unchanged; the unconsumed pairing row still there), `/home/zerops`, `/home/zerops/app`, `/var/www` all kept their markers; **`/tmp` is tmpfs and is wiped**. `state.sqlite` grew 327 680 → 466 944 B with a new sha only because the 4.1 MB WAL was checkpointed on clean shutdown — a sha of the main file alone is not a state fingerprint while the server runs.                                                                 | markers written before; `node:sqlite` row counts + `sha256sum` before/after                                                                |
| **Live nginx edits are erased at boot**: `sudo -E zcp init nginx` re-rendered `nginx.conf` at +13 s and the hand-added `location /z3/` was gone (the `.s0bak` on the rootfs survived). Confirms `map.md`: container-side changes belong in the template.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `nginx -T` after the restart                                                                                                               |
| The pairing chain works again immediately after the restart on the native 3773 origin (mint → token, same five scopes → ticket → `101`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `curl` chain at +2 min                                                                                                                     |
| **Every boot of `t3 serve` mints an unconsumed `administrative-bootstrap` pairing link** with WIDER scopes than a normal mint (136- vs 93-char scope string), 5-min TTL, sitting in `auth_pairing_links` (created 0.5 s after the unit started). Whoever can read the z3 unit's stdout/journal gets an admin credential once per boot — S1 must disable or consume it in the Zerops environment.                                                                                                                                                                                    | `auth_pairing_links` rows via `node:sqlite` right after the restart; `journalctl -u zerops@z3`                                             |
| On `z3probe` the restart re-installed the SAME dev binary (sha unchanged, mtime moved) because its recipe copies `/var/www/zcp-linux-amd64` instead of running `install.sh` — the "upgrade" half of S0.2 is measured on `z3-eval` (see the S0.5 section's last row).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | `sha256sum /usr/local/bin/zcp` before/after                                                                                                |

### S0.10 (redeploy half) — `zcli service push` of `z3probe` with its own recipe (2026-08-28 13:46:21 → 13:48:50 UTC)

| Fact                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | How it was established                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **A redeploy replaces the container and loses everything**: new container `vPjBZfeqTCyWg8wk8Sirgw` (`z3probe-runtime-5-376`, hostname `node-id-4` → `node-id-5`); all five markers gone (`/home/zerops/.t3`, `/home/zerops`, `/home/zerops/app`, `/var/www`, `/tmp`); `/home/zerops/.t3` recreated fresh (`state.sqlite` 4 096 B, 0 threads, 0 sessions, 0 events, all 43 migrations replayed, new `environmentId`); the `zsc`-created unit file gone; `/home/zerops` image-fresh. | `GET /service-stack/{id}/container` before/after; `node:sqlite` row counts; `ls` of every marker path                                                                                                             |
| **Zero downtime during the redeploy**: a 2 s `/healthz` poller (68 samples, `-m 5`) never saw anything but `200` — the L7 cut over from container 4 to 5 seamlessly, versus the restart's ~14 s `502` hole. End to end 2 min 29 s: push 13:46:21 → new container created 13:47:25 → boot 13:47:41 → `initAt` 13:47:49 → `zerops@z3` started 13:47:51 → `/healthz` shows the new `initAt` 13:48:02 → `z3Up:true` 13:48:50.                                                          | `zcli service push --projectId uHqwF26oTQ6V8D86HeABIA --serviceId Nq49woUlTBeFYdP0jg4DQA --setup zcp --no-git --working-dir <dir>` with the live `ZEROPS_YAML` + the binary its `deployFiles` demands; the poller |
| **Cold `npx --yes t3@0.0.35` on an image-fresh container = 58 s** from unit start to `z3Up` (13:47:51 → 13:48:49): H-05's residue, measured — the fresh-project first-boot cost and the argument for the local-bundle-first path (brief §4a).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | unit start time vs `/healthz` `z3Up` flip                                                                                                                                                                         |

### S0.9 (laptop half) — two clients on one container (`z3probe`, native 3773 origin, 2026-08-28 ~13:50 UTC; the phone half is owner-driven, `questions.md` Q-07)

| Fact                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | How it was established                                  |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Two concurrent clients work at the transport level**: two mints → two token exchanges (identical scopes) → two tickets → two `WebSocket`s open at once (A +2.6 s, B +7.8 s), both `readyState 1` after 70 s; opening the second does not close the first; one RPC from each is served independently.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | Node 24 script from the laptop, two sockets             |
| **Wire protocol** (effect `RpcServer.toHttpEffectWebsocket` + `RpcSerialization.layerJson`, `apps/server/src/ws.ts:2488-2523`): a client frame is a JSON ARRAY of messages; `[{"_tag":"Ping"}]` → `{"_tag":"Pong"}` (~13 ms); a request is `{"_tag":"Request","id":"1","tag":"<method>","payload":{},"headers":[]}` → `{"_tag":"Exit","requestId":"1","exit":{"_tag":"Success","value":…}}`. The socket is silent until the client speaks (no hello frame). Method names: `packages/contracts/src/rpc.ts:209` `WS_METHODS`, `orchestration.ts:27` `ORCHESTRATION_WS_METHODS`. `server.getConfig` returns `auth: {policy: "remote-reachable", bootstrapMethods: ["one-time-token"], sessionMethods: ["browser-session-cookie","bearer-access-token","dpop-access-token"], sessionCookieName: "t3_session"}` and `cwd`.                                                                                                                                        | `server.probe` and `server.getConfig` over both sockets |
| **CORS is a wildcard on the z3 server, live**: `OPTIONS /oauth/token` with `Origin: http://localhost:5173` → `204`, `access-control-allow-origin: *`, methods `GET,POST,OPTIONS`, headers `authorization,b3,traceparent,content-type,dpop`; same `*` on a plain GET. Any origin can drive the pairing exchange today (D1's second exposure).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | `curl -i -X OPTIONS` against the 3773 origin            |
| The POC mint's `origin` field is the **3773** URL (first declared port from `zeropsSubdomain`); under D2 it must become `<8080 origin>/z3` or the client pairs to a port that no longer exists.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | `GET /z3-pair/<pw>` response                            |
| **Connecting the current `apps/web` client to a remote environment**: `npm run dev:web` in `../z3` serves the hosted client on port **5733** (`apps/web/vite.config.ts`, `PORT`); a mint with `--base-url <origin>` adds `pairUrl: "<origin>/pair#token=<credential>"`; entry paths (`packages/shared/src/remote.ts:188` `resolveRemotePairingTarget`, params `token`/`host`/`label`): (a) `http://<laptop>:5733/pair?host=<z3 origin>&token=<credential>` on the hosted client's own origin, (b) with zero environments `HostedStaticOnboardingState` (`apps/web/src/routes/_chat.index.tsx:139`) → `/settings/connections` → "Add environment" (`host` + `pairingCode`, `connection/onboarding.ts:35`). Credentials are one-time, 5-min TTL — mint per device right before connecting. `t3 serve` subcommands: `start, serve, pair, auth {pairing,session}, project {add,remove,rename}, service, triage` — no base-path flag, no offline thread creation. | code read at `0e517b5b8`; `pairUrl` verified live       |

### S0.2 (upgrade half) — API restart of `z3-eval`'s `zcp` (platform recipe with `install.sh`; 2026-08-28 14:07:03 UTC, `PUT /service-stack/gt7tJZjDSk2zyH5XvNeAQQ/restart`)

| Fact                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | How it was established                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **A restart IS an upgrade**: the container held the hand-pushed dev binary (`v9.153.0-1-g64f63ae8`, sha `1f0255…`, 46 MB, mtime 12:26:47Z); after the restart `/usr/local/bin/zcp` was the latest RELEASE (`v9.153.0`, sha `57ffe70…`, 13.7 MB, mtime **14:07:12Z** = call +9 s) — `install.sh` re-ran from `initCommands` and replaced it; `zcp init nginx` ran at +13 s. Timeline from the public URL: `302` until the call, first `502` at **+2 s**, `302` again at **+16 s** (~14 s hole, same as z3probe); kernel boot at +6 s (`uptime -s` 14:07:09). | before/after `zcp version`, `sha256sum`, `ls --time-style=full-iso`; a 2 s `curl` poll of `https://zcp-26a7-8080.prg1.zerops.app/`; `journalctl -b` (`sudo … zcp init nginx` at 14:07:16) |
| **A project-scoped token can restart its own service**: the call was made from inside the container with its own `ZCP_API_KEY`; accepted (`200`, process `fimKPqkoQrm6LtpuIwfYbA`, `stack.restart`, `createdByUser` = the token user `zcp-z3-eval`). The product path (S4) uses the user's token, which S0.2 on z3probe showed works too.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | the `PUT` response                                                                                                                                                                        |
| **What survives on the platform recipe**: `/home/zerops/.zcp/z3` (the dev z3 install, symlink + `node_modules`) and `~/.claude/.credentials.json` (the login: `claude -p` → `OK` right after boot); `/var/www` (AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md, the dotdirs). Units `zerops@nginx/vscode/zerops` back; nothing else was registered. Re-push of the dev binary afterwards: `z3-dev-push.sh zcp` only (z3 needed no reinstall).                                                                                                                                         | `ls`, `claude -p "Reply with exactly OK" --output-format json`, `systemctl list-units`, the push log                                                                                      |
| Side fact: `sudo -E zsc unit remove <name>` deletes the unit file but leaves systemd with a stale `failed` record (`LoadState=loaded`, `UnitFileState=bad`, or `not-found failed`) until `systemctl daemon-reload` + `reset-failed` (or a reboot). Anything reading `systemctl list-units` for mount state (`ops/mount.go` does) can see a ghost after an unmount.                                                                                                                                                                                          | `systemctl show zerops@sshfs-s0git3` after the S0.3 cleanup, cleared with `daemon-reload` + `reset-failed`                                                                                |

### S6 PROVE — envelope on the wire: `structuredContent` vs text (live on `z3-eval`, 2026-08-28 14:39 UTC, temporary zcp patch returning the envelope as the handler's typed output; reverted)

| Fact                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | How it was established                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| The Go MCP SDK (`go-sdk v1.6.1`, `mcp/server.go:355-390`) marshals a non-nil typed handler output into `structuredContent` and leaves the text block intact: raw JSON-RPC `tools/call zerops_workflow {action:"status"}` → `content: [1 text block, 3 649 B]` + `structuredContent` (233 B, keys `phase, environment, idleScenario, selfService, project, services, generated`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | `printf` initialize/initialized/tools-call into `zcp serve` on the container, `python3` over the reply                                                                                                        |
| **Claude Code 2.1.250 REPLACES the model-facing tool result with `structuredContent` when it is present.** The `user` event's `tool_result.content` became the 216-byte JSON string of the envelope — the 3.6 KB markdown guidance never reached the model; the stream additionally carries `tool_use_result: {content: "<json>", structuredContent: {…}}`. So `structuredContent` cannot carry the envelope beside the LLM text: it would strip every atom the status renders. **Decision: the envelope rides inside the text** (a fenced JSON block after the markdown), which both Claude Code (`item.completed` raw block) and Codex (`mcpToolCall.result.content`) forward verbatim. | `claude -p '…zerops_workflow action="status"…' --output-format stream-json --verbose --allowedTools mcp__zerops__zerops_workflow` on the container; `python3` over the jsonl                                  |
| **Envelope-in-text verified live** (zcp `343bd2d2`, branch `feat/z3-envelope-wire`, pushed to `z3-eval` 14:57 UTC): the `zerops_workflow action="status"` tool result reaching Claude Code is one 3 892-byte text block that still starts with `## Status` (the atoms reach the model) and ends with a fenced `json zcp-envelope` block that parses back to the `StateEnvelope` (`phase`, `environment`, `idleScenario`, `selfService`, `project`, `services`, `generated`); `structuredContent` appears nowhere in the stream. Block size 140 B idle, ~1.7 KB with 4 services + a work session; the 32 KB MCP response cap has ~8 KB headroom above `ComposeBodyBudget` (24 KB).         | `claude -p 'Call zerops_workflow action="status" and then stop.' --output-format stream-json --verbose --allowedTools mcp__zerops__zerops_workflow` on the container, python regex over the last fenced block |

### S0.9 (web half) — two web clients on one z3 server (dev fork `t3@0.0.35-dev.0e517b5b8` started by hand on `z3-eval`'s zcp, loopback :3773 + SSH tunnel, cwd `/var/www`, real `claudeAgent` provider under the copied login; 2026-08-28 ~15:00 UTC; cleaned up)

| Fact                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | How it was established                                                                                                                        |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Two independent web clients share one thread, live.** Client A (own Vite origin, own pairing) created project `s09-var-www` (`workspaceRoot /var/www`) + a thread and got a real agent reply (`PONG`, turn done server-side in ~4.7 s). Client B (second Vite instance on another port ⇒ separate origin/localStorage, its own fresh credential) saw the project + thread with a live-activity dot right after pairing, with identical content. A new message dispatched into the thread by a third socket authenticated as A appeared in B's already-open view by **live push in ~502 ms** (DOM polled, no reload), the agent's reply followed. Three concurrent connections, zero eviction or "thread busy". Former Q-07 answered. | puppeteer-driven browser + browser-console WS calls (`orchestration.dispatchCommand` — the payload IS the command object, not `{command: …}`) |
| **Correction to the row above ("Connecting the current `apps/web` client…")**: `npm run dev:web` is the dev-runner harness — it wires the client to a co-located LOCAL backend and every route incl. `/pair?host=&token=` redirects into a local-pairing gate that ignores the query. The hosted-client behaviour is `cd apps/web && VITE_HOSTED_APP_CHANNEL=nightly PORT=<p> npm run dev` (no `VITE_HTTP_URL`/`VITE_WS_URL`) — then `isHostedStaticApp()` is true and `http://<origin>/pair?host=<z3 origin>&token=<credential>` pairs first try.                                                                                                                                                                                     | tried both                                                                                                                                    |
| **`auth.policy` is derived only from the server's `--host` bind string** (`apps/server/src/auth/EnvironmentAuthPolicy.ts` + `utils.ts` `isRemoteReachableHost`): `127.0.0.1`/`localhost`/`::1` ⇒ `loopback-browser` regardless of a proxy/tunnel in front. Behind nginx on loopback (the S2 layout) the server would mis-classify itself; the bearer → ticket → WS chain still worked under that label. S1 overrides the policy in Zerops mode.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | `server.getConfig` over the tunnel + code read                                                                                                |
| **Bug**: the GUI "Add project" (folder-browse) dialog hangs on "Connecting…" indefinitely in this setup while raw `server.probe`/`server.getConfig`/`orchestration.dispatchCommand` over the same bearer answer instantly — one UI flow, not transport/auth. `questions.md` Q-13. Worked around by dispatching the create-project command directly; S2's `--auto-bootstrap-project-from-cwd` avoids the dialog on the product path anyway.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | ~40 s wait in the browser; console RPCs                                                                                                       |
