### Q-10 (answered) + S4 API facts (laptop, 2026-08-28 15:05–15:20 UTC)

| Fact                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | How it was established                                                                                                                     |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| **Zerops' Turnstile site key is hostname-bound: a page on any non-Zerops origin cannot obtain a token.** The production GUI uses `0x4AAAAAABkfI4SNvJav8428` (same as `environment.local.ts`; resolved from the live challenge-platform request on `app.zerops.io/registration`, the source files carry only a build placeholder). A minimal page with that key on `http://localhost:8765` fails at the widget with Cloudflare error **110200 "Domain not authorized"** before any token exists. In-z3 registration therefore needs a platform-side change (z3's hostnames added to the key's allowed domains) — Q-10 answered: no. | puppeteer on `app.zerops.io/registration` (`performance.getEntriesByType('resource')`) and on the local page; Cloudflare's error-code docs |
| **`GET /client/{clientId}/project` is lag-free; the search trails by ~0.5 s.** After `zcli project create` (LIGHT), the direct read returned the new project (`status: CREATING`) **498 ms** after the create was issued — ~600 ms before the create call itself returned — while `POST /project/search` needed 994 ms (one clean sample; a sequential-poll run read 1 853 vs 1 953 ms). A project row exists long before `ACTIVE`. The endpoint takes `statuses`, `limit`, `offset`, `nameContains` (`ListClientProjects`).                                                                                                       | two throwaway projects `s4probe`/`s4probe2` in org KRLS, created and deleted; 250 ms parallel polls                                        |
| **`POST /auth/refresh` returns the session fields at the TOP level** (`ResponseAuthFull`), unlike `/auth/login` (`ResponseAuthLogin`) and `/2fa/totp/login` which wrap them in `auth` — a client reading `body.auth` after a refresh silently signs the user out.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | spec + the S4.1 client tests                                                                                                               |
| Correction to the S0.11 row: `zcp studio topology` DOES emit **`mountPath`** (`/var/www/<hostname>`) for a service whose mount directory exists (`internal/tools/discover.go:127-132`, on the exported `EnrichWithMetaStatus` path) — the repo enumerator for S3/S6 rests on it. Managed types read like `valkey:single@7.2`, runtimes carry the OS prefix (`ubuntu/nodejs@22`); `adoptionState` values seen live: `zcp-self`, `adoptable`, `managed-dep`.                                                                                                                                                                         | live sample with a mounted `nodejs@22`, a valkey and an unmounted runtime, 2026-08-28 15:06 UTC                                            |

## Streams — live-verified on `z3-eval` (2026-08-28)

### S1 — the door (fork `z3-s1` at `ff5734de8`, merged into `z3` as `65487b468`; server started by hand on loopback with `T3CODE_ZEROPS_PROJECT_ID`/`T3CODE_ZEROPS_API_HOST`, reached through an SSH tunnel)

| Fact                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | How it was established                               |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------- |
| **`T3CODE_ZEROPS_PROJECT_ID` set ⇒ the descriptor flips**: `GET /api/auth/session` → `auth.policy: "remote-reachable"` (regardless of the loopback bind), `bootstrapMethods: ["zerops-identity","one-time-token"]`; without the variable the same binary answers `loopback-browser` / `["one-time-token"]` — every Zerops behaviour is gated by the env presence, not by where the request came from.                                          | check 1 and check 6 (second server without the vars) |
| **`POST /api/auth/zerops-identity {token}` is the door**: owner's token → `200` `{id, credential (12 chars, upstream's pairing shape), label: "Zerops OWNER", expiresAt (+2 min)}` in 0.10 s; the non-member's token → `403` `{_tag: EnvironmentOperationForbiddenError, code: operation_forbidden, reason: zerops_project_membership_required}` with no credential; `not-a-token` → `401` `{code: auth_invalid, reason: invalid_credential}`. | checks 2–4 from the laptop through the tunnel        |
| **The exchanged session is the membership window**: `POST /oauth/token` (token-exchange) → `expires_in: 899`, the five standard scopes, `token_type: Bearer` — not the 30-day one-time-token TTL; `POST /api/auth/websocket-ticket` → 192-char ticket; `ws://…/ws?wsTicket=…` + `server.getConfig` → `Exit/Success` with the same `auth` block. A member reaches a thread-capable socket with no pairing code (S1 acceptance line).            | check 5                                              |
| Runbook note: `pgrep -af bin.mjs` does not match the `.bin/t3` process — match on `t3 serve`; `nohup … &` inside one ssh call does not detach reliably — use `ssh -f` or `setsid … </dev/null`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | cleanup                                              |

### S2 (zcp half, slices 1–2) — `zcp init` supervises z3 (zcp `03682093` on branch `feat/z3-init`, pushed to `z3-eval` 15:16 UTC)

| Fact                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | How it was established                                                                                                  |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **A plain `zcp init` brings z3 up**: step `Zerops Code (z3)` used the bundle already at `~/.zcp/z3` (no npm, no network), wrote `~/.zcp/z3.env` (0600: `T3CODE_ZEROPS_PROJECT_ID`, `T3CODE_ZEROPS_API_HOST`), registered `zerops@z3` via `sudo -E zsc unit create z3 "zcp service start z3"` (unit file `/usr/lib/systemd/system/zerops@z3.service`, enabled), wrote the marker `/var/www/.zcp/state/init-complete` = `{"initComplete":true,"initAt":…}`, and exited 0. 10 s later `http://127.0.0.1:3773/.well-known/t3/environment` answered and `/api/auth/session` showed `policy: remote-reachable`, `bootstrapMethods: ["zerops-identity","one-time-token"]` — the env file reached the supervised process. A second `zcp init` registered nothing (idempotent). | the push loop's `zcp init`, then a hand-run second `zcp init`; `systemctl status zerops@z3`; curls inside the container |
| The base-path probe is honest: with a bundle that predates `--base-path` the init log prints `! the installed z3 bundle does not advertise --base-path; z3 will answer under /z3/ but its assets will not resolve` and the supervisor omits the flag.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | init output on the container                                                                                            |

### S2 (zcp half, slice 3) + S1.4 — the public `/z3/` door through nginx (zcp `6b998342` on `z3-eval`, z3 `z3-s1` at `8051697c8` under `zerops@z3`; 15:21–15:25 UTC)

| Fact                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | How it was established                                  |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------- |
| **`GET /healthz` on the public 8080 origin is static and gate-free**: `200 application/json`, `cache-control: no-store`, body `{"initComplete":true,"initAt":"2026-08-28T15:21:25Z"}` (the marker `zcp init` wrote); code-server's own `/healthz` is shadowed; `/` still `302 /zcp-login`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | `curl -i https://zcp-26a7-8080.prg1.zerops.app/healthz` |
| **`location /z3/` proxies z3 outside the cookie gate**: `/z3/.well-known/t3/environment` → `200 application/json` (`serverVersion 0.0.35`, no `basePath` yet — the installed bundle predates the flag); `/z3/api/auth/session` → `policy: remote-reachable`, `bootstrapMethods: ["zerops-identity","one-time-token"]`. During the unit's restart the location answers `502` (the L7 error page) for ~8 s.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | curls from the laptop, no cookie                        |
| **code-server's port-proxy door is closed for the z3 port only**: `/proxy/3773/` → `404`, `/absproxy/3773/` → `404`, `/proxy/8081/x` → `302` (login) as before.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | curls from the laptop                                   |
| **S1.4's CORS allowlist holds through nginx**: `OPTIONS /z3/oauth/token` with `Origin: https://evil.example` → no `access-control-allow-origin` (the S0.9 wildcard is gone); `http://localhost:5733` → echoed; `http://127.0.0.1:5733` → none (trusted by hostname, like the welcome bridge).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | curls from the laptop                                   |
| Until the S2 server/web slice is pushed the SPA at `/z3/` still references `/assets/…` (root-absolute) — the S0.5 finding, unchanged.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | `curl -s …/z3/ \| grep src=`                            |
| **S1.4 through the public door** (fork `8051697c8` under `zerops@z3`, via nginx `/z3/`): a VALID ticket with `Origin: https://evil.example` on `/z3/ws` → `403` `{code: operation_forbidden, reason: origin_not_allowed}` — refused before the ticket is read; the container's own origin → `400` (upgrade attempted, curl is not a websocket); no `Origin` → `400` likewise; Node's `WebSocket` (no Origin) to `wss://…/z3/ws?wsTicket=…` opens and `server.getConfig` answers. CORS preflight on `/z3/oauth/token`: evil → no allow-origin header, `http://localhost:5733` → echoed, `http://127.0.0.1:5733` → none. The non-member's token at the public door → `403 zerops_project_membership_required`. | s1-verify pack from the laptop, 15:24 UTC               |

### S2 (z3 half) — base path end to end (fork `z3-s2` at `b72194310`, merged into `z3`; verified on a local build, live check pending the next push)

| Fact                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | How it was established                                                                                                                                                      |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **nginx strips, the server only knows its public prefix**: `t3 serve --base-path /z3` (or `T3CODE_BASE_PATH`) makes the descriptor advertise `"basePath":"/z3"` and every absolute URL the server emits join it (`pairUrl` → `<origin>/z3/pair#token=…`); routes stay at `/`. The web bundle bakes the prefix at build time: `VITE_BASE_PATH=/z3 vp run --filter @t3tools/web build` (the push loop forwards `Z3_BASE_PATH`); `index.html` uses `%BASE_URL%`, the manifest went relative, TanStack router got `basepath`, the window-origin target is origin+prefix. Default build is byte-shape identical to upstream.                       | local build served on `127.0.0.1:3799`: descriptor JSON with `basePath`, all six shell assets `200` under `/z3/`, `/thread/42` → the shell, the full auth chain + `/ws` 101 |
| **12 client sites dropped a path prefix, not the 10 the plan mapped** — the two unmapped ones were the worst: `packages/client-runtime/src/rpc/http.ts` `remoteApiBaseUrl` (the real request base of every API call) and the DPoP `htu` builder (a proof bound to a URL the request never reaches); plus `state/assets.ts` (every image/attachment would 404), `settings/pairingUrls.ts`, mobile `connection/pairing.ts` and `cloud/linkEnvironment.ts`. Six upstream tests that pinned "a base URL's path is noise" now assert the prefix survives.                                                                                          | S2z-1/S2z-2 commits + tests                                                                                                                                                 |
| **A tolerant ingress strip is structurally impossible**: any middleware passed to effect's `HttpRouter.serve` leaves its `R` unsolvable (499 type errors, three typings tried). Instead a request still carrying the configured prefix gets a **404 JSON naming the mistake** (proxy must strip; `proxy_pass` needs its trailing slash); unmatched `/api`, `/oauth`, `/.well-known`, `/ws` are 404 JSON in production too (previously dev-only, so they returned 200 HTML). S0.5's silent mode is dead; consequence: code-server's `/proxy/3773/` and direct-to-3773 browsing do not work with a `/z3/`-built bundle — use the `/z3/` origin. | `b72194310`; local `/z3/.well-known/t3/environment` on the unstripped path → 404 with that text                                                                             |

### S3 — live audit on `z3-eval` (fork `beef10c47`→`4d0230b4a` under `zerops@z3`, rig `s3git1`/`s3git2` docusaurus + throwaway `s3git3` node repo with 19 308 untracked `node_modules` paths and no `.gitignore`; 2026-08-28 16:18–17:1x UTC; 6 940 argv samples at 20 Hz)

| Fact                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | How it was established                                                                                    |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **The S3 invariant holds**: zero bare `git` processes on the container during a two-service turn; zero argv carrying a `/var/www/<host>` path; every git call is `ssh -l zerops -o ControlMaster=auto -o ControlPath=/tmp/ssh-mux-%r@%h:%p -o ControlPersist=600 -o BatchMode=yes -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o LogLevel=ERROR -o ServerAliveInterval=15 -o ServerAliveCountMax=3 <host> env GIT_INDEX_FILE=/var/www/.git/t3-checkpoint-index-<uuid> GIT_AUTHOR_NAME=… git -C /var/www …` (only `GIT_*` cross the wire — no `PATH`, no `ZCP_API_KEY`, no `GIT_TRACE2*`). Two-service turn 24.9 s wall incl. agent thinking; one ControlMaster per host, created once and reused across the session.                                                           | `ps -eo args` sampler at 20 Hz during a WS-driven turn; `ssh -O check`                                    |
| **sshd `MaxSessions` (default 10) is not a limit**: 16 concurrent sessions over one master → 16/16 ok in 3 s (sequential would be 48 s); the cap of 4 per host has room.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | 16-way parallel probe on `s3git1`                                                                         |
| **Policy live**: a `thread.create` carrying a `worktreePath` persisted `worktreePath: null`; capabilities `pullRequests:false`, `vcsStackedActions:false` (`repositoryIdentity:true`); 0 `git fetch` in 6 940 samples.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | read model + capabilities + sampler                                                                       |
| **Multi-repo checkpoints live**: one turn editing both services wrote the identical ref name `refs/t3/checkpoints/<thread>/turn/1` on BOTH hosts with different oids per repo; the turn diff `files[]` = `s3git1/README.md`, `s3git2/README.md` (prefixed, sorted); restore fanned out with each repo's own source (`restore --source <oid>` + `reset --quiet`), zero `git clean`, and an untracked file planted on the host after the checkpoint survived the revert.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | `for-each-ref refs/t3` on both hosts; the turn diff; `?? uploads-audit.txt` after restore                 |
| **Restore visibility through the mount is immediate**: host and mount showed the reverted tracked files in the same 10 Hz poll, 6.27 s after dispatch (the fan-out itself, not mount lag). S0.4's 20 s applies to directory listings and stale entries, not to rewritten tracked files.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | 10 Hz poll on both sides                                                                                  |
| **The native FileFinder skips `node_modules` on its own — S3.5 is unnecessary**: with 32 764 dirents under `/var/www` (incl. a 19 308-file `node_modules` in a repo with no `.gitignore`), `waitForIndexReady(15000)` → `ok:true` in 2 027 / 3 646 / 1 979 ms; results are mount-relative (`s3git1/…`) with a `gitStatus` per file; `s3git3` is indexed but no `node_modules` path is ever returned (`lodash`, present only there, finds nothing).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | `FileFinder.create({basePath:"/var/www"})` probes on the container, 3 runs                                |
| **The `add -A` guard FAILED live**: the probe `ls-files --others --exclude-standard -z` ran over SSH (1 165 676 bytes, 19 308 paths) but `s3git3` still received turn/0 and turn/1 and the turn/0 tree holds 19 310 files — `node_modules` went into the checkpoint. Locally the truncation, the guard and the refusal all work against a real repo with 6 000 untracked files (`ZeropsUntrackedProbe.test.ts`), so the defect is specific to the SSH path (overflow not reported, or the probe failing into a swallowed `false`). Second defect by reading: `probeUntrackedOverflow` caches the VISIT, not the verdict — a repo would be refused at most once per server lifetime. Both being fixed. `git status --porcelain` shows one line `?? node_modules/` for all of it (S0.3's point). | the audit turn on `s3git3`; `ls-tree -r` of the checkpoint; `z3-s3` `c3239b441`                           |
| **Nothing prunes checkpoint refs on thread delete** (the brief's checkpoint rule 3): the audit's thread left `refs/t3/checkpoints/<thread>/turn/*` on both hosts — fan-out pruning being added.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | `for-each-ref` after cleanup                                                                              |
| **Guard root cause was memoisation, not SSH**: `probeUntrackedOverflow` cached the visit (`probed.has(cwd)`), and the reactor drives the baseline TWICE per turn (`turn-start-requested` and `message-sent`, `CheckpointReactor.ts:854`) through one fan-out — first call refused, second call skipped the probe and captured the tree it had just refused; the completion capture followed. Fixed by probing every time (one `ls-files` capped at 256 KB, ~0.1 s over the mux); caching the verdict was rejected because a repo would stay refused after its `.gitignore` arrives. A whole-stack test with a `/bin/sh`-executing fake `ssh` (`ZeropsGuardOverSsh.test.ts`) shows the spawner path was correct all along. Live re-run pending the next push.                                   | `z3-s3` `031803591`; RED "keeps refusing while the repository still overflows, however often it is asked" |

Cards did not render on this pass because the container's build predates the server-side projection seam — the degrade path ("Used 2 tools") worked as designed.                                                                                                                                                                                                     | the same paired session, `z3-s6ui` `0c3ef1033`                                                                                                      |
| **Resolution of the "subscription never pushes" rows above — the probe, not the feed.** effect-rpc streams are flow-controlled per request: the server writes a Chunk, closes a latch and waits for the client's `Ack` (`RpcServer.ts:401-421`, opened at `:191-193`; frame `[{"_tag":"Ack","requestId":"<id>"}]`, `RpcMessage.ts:96-122`). A raw WS probe that never acks receives exactly ONE Chunk and no Exit on a healthy socket; `RpcClient` acks automatically, so every real client works. Live from inside the container with the Ack line added: subscribe → Chunk; `zcli` import of a valkey → a Chunk containing it **0.5 s** after the import started, then settling Chunks (doorbell push as designed, `doorbellConnected:true`). Runbook: every raw probe of `subscribe*` methods must ack each Chunk.                                            | `z3-s6c` `c8e7c87d3` (two tests: publish from an RPC handler fiber; a late second subscriber); the acked probe on the container, 17:29 UTC          |
| **Card decoders hold against byte-exact live payloads** (`zerops_verify` 901 B → `{kind:"verify", hostname:"s3git1", status:"healthy", checks:[service_running pass, error_logs pass]}`; a failed `zerops_deploy` 2 048 B → the error card with `SSH_DEPLOY_FAILED`, suggestion, `failureClass:"network"`), captured by asking the agent to paste the result text verbatim (the client only ever sees the 84-char summary). Two facts the Go structs did not show: a live error message is MULTI-LINE (zcli's log embedded — five lines), and a verify document carries `workSessionState` AND a top-level `envelope` beside the `VerifyResult` fields — the card ignores both on purpose (the envelope reaches the client only through the lifecycle feed, so a card and the strip can never disagree).                                                         | `z3-s6ui` `0ddc3f878`, `apps/web/src/zerops/cards/liveFixtures.ts`                                                                                  |
| **Lifecycle persistence in the wild**: the z3 unit restarted mid-turn (a push) — "Provider session did not survive a server restart" — and the strip kept reading `infrastructure ready · 2 services` straight through it (the persisted latest envelope, S6 server D6).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | the same thread across the 17:32 UTC restart                                                                                                        |
| **Former Q-14 — the adopt gate on `zerops_deploy` is by design skipped until the FIRST adoption in a project**: `requireAdoption` (`internal/tools/guard.go:83-134`) returns nil while `<stateDir>/services/` does not exist ("gate activates once the first service is adopted, a clean migration path"); on `z3-eval` no bootstrap ever ran, so `zerops_deploy targetService="s3git1"` reached zcli and failed with `SSH_DEPLOY_FAILED` (`File zerops.yml not found`; its `failureClassification.category:"network"` is the transport default — a separate mislabel). The S6 envelope slice (`d6e4db11`) only appends `Envelope` to success responses after the deploy — not a regression. The mismatch is `zerops_discover`'s unconditional `ADOPT_REQUIRED` promise (`internal/tools/discover.go:258`); tightening the gate or the wording is an owner call. | code read + `claude -p` reproduction on the container, zcp `21421bb4`                                                                               |
| **The `add -A` guard holds live after the fix** (fork `2415aa536`): `s3git3` recreated with no `.gitignore` and 18 380 untracked paths (1.1 MB of `ls-files -z`); one turn appending to all three READMEs settled in 19.6 s; `s3git1`/`s3git2` got turn/0 + turn/1, **`s3git3` got 0 refs**, the turn diff lists only the two, and the thread carries the activity `No checkpoint for /var/www/s3git3: This repository has thousands of untracked files and no usable .gitignore…`. Argv (5 552 samples): the probe ran per capture (8 samples), zero `add -A`/`write-tree`/`commit-tree`/`update-ref` against `s3git3`, zero argv on a mount path. | **Checkpoint refs survive `thread.delete`** (confirmed live): immediately after deleting the audit threads, both hosts still carried 5 of their `refs/t3/checkpoints/<thread>/turn/*` — pruning on delete being added (S3).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `for-each-ref refs/t3` on both hosts after the RPC deletes                                                                                          |
| **A one-time-token pairing dies after 15 minutes in a Zerops project**: S1's membership window (`expires_in` 899) caps every session issued while `T3CODE_ZEROPS_PROJECT_ID` is set — including sessions from `t3 auth pairing create` (which have no Zerops token to re-mint with) and DPoP's 1 h default. Observed: a hosted client paired at 17:19 UTC showed "The environment credential is invalid" at ~17:34, Reconnect did not clear it, a reload lost the strip too (the client cache) — misread at first as the push invalidating sessions. But the thread defaults to **Codex** (`getAutoBootstrapDefaultModelSelection` is hardcoded `codex` + `DEFAULT_MODEL` upstream, `serverRuntimeStartup.ts:172`), which is unauthenticated here, and the model picker shows "No models found" — a product defect for the landing; fix: default to the first authenticated provider (Claude on these containers).                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | S6 UI pairing at 17:41 UTC; `projection_projects` via `node:sqlite`                                                                                 |
| **Cards live** (fork `cfbed0289`): the verify card renders `s3git1 · healthy` with two green checks; the deploy-error card renders `SSH_DEPLOY_FAILED` with its seven zcli log lines as separate lines (the multi-line fix in the wild), the diagnostic hint and a `network` badge; the strip sits clear of the panel controls; the prose `zerops_workflow status` result gets no card (by design — the envelope reaches the client only via the lifecycle feed). Correction to an earlier reading: the model picker is not empty — it shows "No models found" until the user types (typing "claude" lists nine models). A bounded wait for resolved provider statuses is the follow-up.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | `projection_projects`/`projection_threads` via `node:sqlite` 14 s after the restart                                                                 |

Root cause of the earlier miss: every managed provider publishes a synchronous "probe pending" snapshot (`enabled && !installed && status==="warning"`, the registry's own `isPendingInitialProbe`) and the real probe runs on a forked fibre — the bootstrap now waits (≤ 30 s, 250 ms polls of `getProviders`) until NO provider is still probing, then picks Claude → Codex → any ready; an empty registry is not decidable; a settled registry costs one read. Outside a Zerops environment the upstream Codex default is byte-identical.                                                                                                 | `projection_projects`/`projection_threads` via `node:sqlite` 40 s after the restart; `ZeropsBootstrapModel.test.ts`, `serverRuntimeStartup.test.ts` |

Timed 20-minute survival probes running.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | pack 6, T0 = 18:02:19 UTC                                                                                                                           |

## Freeze — the hard fork (laptop, 2026-08-28 ~20:00 UTC)

| Fact                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | How it was established                                                                       |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Frozen upstream base = `f94a0d646`** (`upstream/main` on 2026-08-28), tag `upstream-base-2026-08-28`; the product branch `z3` became `main` (`c537c53f4` at the rename); the fork's previous `main` (8 POC-era commits: brand, POC Zerops page, fork README, iOS build brief) is preserved as tag `main-poc-2026-08-28` and is contained in `zerops-poc`. `origin/z3` remains as an alias until deleted by hand.                                                                                                                                                          | `git tag`, `git branch -f main z3`, `git merge-base --is-ancestor`; pushed                   |
| **Provider work is not self-contained**: of 85 upstream commits touching `apps/server/src/provider`, `packages/effect-codex-app-server`, `packages/effect-acp` in the 60 days before the freeze, **37 (44 %) also change non-test code outside those paths** — `apps/web/src/components` 56 files, `apps/mobile/src/features` 34, `apps/server/src/orchestration` 33, `packages/client-runtime/src/state` 21, `contracts/settings.ts` 10, `contracts/orchestration.ts` 8 (vendored `.repos/`, tests, docs, lockfile excluded). Basis of the import/port split in `fork.md`. | `git log upstream/main --since='60 days ago' -- <paths>` + `git show --name-only` per commit |
| Upstream velocity around the freeze: 117 / 156 / 197 / 127 non-merge commits in the four weeks before 2026-08-28; 51 of the last 28 days' commits touched the provider layer. `v0.0.35` (2026-08-26) is the last stable tag; `v0.0.36` exists only as nightlies.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `git log upstream/main --since/--until`, `git tag --sort=-creatordate`                       |
| Our surface at the freeze: 120 new files, 100 modified upstream files (about half tests), **0 modified and 0 new under the provider paths above**.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | `git diff --name-status upstream-base-2026-08-28...main`                                     |

### S7 — the OAuth flag's `sensitive` visibility (laptop + `z3-eval`, 2026-08-29 morning)

| Fact                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | How it was established                                                                                                                                            |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **A `sensitive=true` service user-data entry is invisible to the Zerops GUI's agent-flag read.** The GUI reads flags from its `UserDataEntity` store = `POST /user-data/search`, where a sensitive entry comes back with `content:"REDACTED"` even for the org OWNER token; `serviceStack.userData` (stack GET) and `activeAppVersion.userDataList` carry ONLY SYSTEM entries (9 / 18 on `z3-eval`'s zcp) — no USER entry, sensitive or not. Verbatim content of a sensitive entry appears only on `GET /service-stack/{id}/env`, `GET /service-stack/{id}/user-data`, `GET /user-data/{id}`, which the GUI's flag path does not use. Consequence: a flag written by `zcp agent mark-oauth` (sensitive=true at the time) renders as NOT authorized in the GUI → S7-0 makes the writer non-sensitive. | probe with the container's integration token and the OWNER token; org-wide scan of 1 371 user-data entries (747 SYSTEM, 621 USER non-sensitive, 3 USER sensitive) |
| All 19 `ZCP_AGENT_OAUTH_*` entries in org KRLS are `sensitive:false` (GUI-written: `POST /api/service-stack/{id}/user-data {key, content:'true'}` with no `sensitive` field). `z3-eval`'s zcp carries `ZCP_AGENT_OAUTH_CLAUDE_CODE=true` + `ZCP_AGENT_AUTH_TYPE_CLAUDE_CODE=oauth`, both non-sensitive, both present in `/etc/zerops-zembed/env.json`; `zcp agent mark-oauth claude-code` there → `{"ok":true,"agent":"claude-code","key":"ZCP_AGENT_OAUTH_CLAUDE_CODE","changed":false}`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | same probe                                                                                                                                                        |
| **The FE import path now produces sensitive entries the GUI then cannot read back**: service `zcp` in project `zerops-code` (created 2026-08-28 15:53 via the FE with `envSecrets`) has `VSCODE_PASSWORD`, `ZCP_VSCODE`, `ZCP_VSCODE_AUTH_ENABLED` as `sensitive:true` → `hasVscode` reads `REDACTED` (inferred from code + API data; GUI rendering not observed). `z3-eval` (pool-shaped, 10:57) and `localflow` (July) have all of them non-sensitive. Platform/GUI item for the owner.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | org scan + `zerops-services.utils.ts:207-258` read-through                                                                                                        |
| Gotchas: the container's `ZEROPS_ServiceId` is NOT the API service id (404 `serviceStackNotFound`) — `ZEROPS_StackId` is; `POST /user-data/search` needs a `clientId` filter or answers 400; an ES bool filter `{"name":"sensitive","operator":"eq"}` → 400 (filter client-side). The container's integration token shows `NO_ACCESS` on the client in `/user/info` but reads the whole project.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | same probe                                                                                                                                                        |

### S7-1 — watcher + scheduler findings (laptop, 2026-08-29, this repo's pinned Effect)

| Fact                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | How it was established                                   |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Interrupting an Effect-wrapped `fs.watch` stream (`FileSystem.watch` + `Effect.forkScoped`) via scope closure crashes the scheduler**: `TypeError: self.addObserver is not a function` inside `MixedScheduler`, whether or not a watch event ever fired. A second shape hits the same crash with no `fs.watch` at all: two forked fibers racing (one draining a `PubSub`, one debounce-driven doing a `FileSystem` op). Consequence: the S7 credential watcher is plain Node `fs.watch` (`ZeropsAgentAuthWatcher.ts`, `watchWithFallback`), bridged into Effect through a `Queue` (`Queue.offerUnsafe` → one forked, debounced drainer) — that bridge does not hit the bug. | minimal repros in isolation; `ZeropsAgentAuthIo.test.ts` |
| A `Schedule.exponential` retry nested inside a fiber already woken from `Stream.debounce`'s clock wait silently gives up after one attempt (only when nested that way; the retry is correct standalone). The "`ZeropsCliFailed` retries then succeeds" case is therefore not unit-tested — S7-3 live is the proof.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | repro + the removed flaky test's comment                 |
| `it.layer(NodeServices.layer)` merges a virtual `TestClock` by default in this repo's `@effect/vitest` setup — anything using real timers needs `{ excludeTestServices: true }`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | `ZeropsAgentAuthIo.test.ts`                              |
| Watching a lone FILE with `fs.watch` misses "change" events on this machine (Node's documented caveat); watch the parent directory filtered by basename instead — the same tolerant attach welcome.js uses (fall back to the parent until the target exists, then re-attach).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | live on the laptop; `ZeropsAgentAuthWatcher.test.ts`     |
| Streaming zerops RPCs are named flat camelCase after their key (`subscribeZeropsTopology`, `subscribeZeropsLifecycle`, now `subscribeZeropsAgentAuth`); the dotted form is only for non-streaming get/refresh verbs. The `WsRpcGroup` scope map in `auth/RpcAuthorization.ts` is compile-time exhaustive — a new route needs its scope entry.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | `packages/contracts/src/rpc.ts`, `RpcAuthorization.ts`   |

### SPI live sanity on `z3-eval` (`t3@0.1.0-dev.900e03d9a`, laptop, 2026-08-29 09:33–09:45 UTC)

| Fact                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | How it was established                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **The SPI refactor (owned bus + `toolCall` enrichment) keeps the lifecycle path intact end to end**: a real Claude turn (`claude-sonnet-5`, prompt `Call zerops_workflow action="status", then call zerops_mount action="status", then reply done.`, 14.9 s) produced lifecycle chunks `zerops_workflow inProgress` → `+zerops_mount inProgress` → `zerops_workflow completed + envelope` (87 ms after the server's completion stamp, 88 ms BEFORE the thread projection's own completed item) → `zerops_mount completed`, the latest envelope winning; `recentTools` updated in place per `itemId` (2 entries, never 4); the topology feed re-read on completion (`readAt` advanced; `zcp studio topology` spawns at the 3 s cadence for the 90 s nudge window) and re-emitted nothing because nothing changed (`contentSignature` excludes `readAt`, by design); journal: zero enrichment / unrecognized / WARN lines for the two Claude MCP items. | door → WS (`subscribeZeropsTopology`, `subscribeZeropsLifecycle`, `orchestration.dispatchCommand thread.create/turn.start/thread.delete`), 2 Hz `ps` sampler on the container; artifacts in the session scratch |
| Day-1 door recipe unchanged: `POST /z3/api/auth/zerops-identity` (134 ms) → `POST /z3/oauth/token` token-exchange (53 ms, `expires_in` 899, scopes incl. `exec:operate`) → `POST /z3/api/auth/websocket-ticket` → `wss://…/z3/ws?wsTicket=…`; Ping every 5 s, Ack after every Chunk.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | same run                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Ordering worth knowing for the web: the lifecycle feed publishes ~90 ms before the thread projection's completed item — a strip driven by the lifecycle feed updates before the card for the same tool.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | same run                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Rig drift since day 1: `weatherdash` (alpine/nginx, adopted, mounted) exists → envelope `idleScenario: bootstrapped`, 3 services; residue z3 projects (three `www` rows, `s6-lifecycle-probe(-2)`, `s3-audit`, 12 threads); upstream's `thread.delete` is a soft delete (`deletedAt`), the snapshot still lists the row.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | `GET /api/orchestration/snapshot`, `zerops.topology.get`                                                                                                                                                        |

### S7-3 — agent auth live on `z3-eval` (zcp `6be2f654`, `t3@0.1.0-dev.b2d5d044f`, 2026-08-29 09:46–10:11 UTC)

| Fact                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | How it was established                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **The agent-auth feed reacts to the credential file within half a second**: `mv ~/.claude/.credentials.json` aside → `credPresent:false`, `state:reconnect` in 357 / 351 ms; back → `credPresent:true`, `state:authorized` in 467 / 378 ms; the picker's provider feed follows with a fresh Claude check 1.7 s (probe cache hit) / 5.0 s (real probe ≈3.5 s) after the restore. `zcp agent mark-oauth` was not spawned during the session — the per-process `markedOAuth` latch was set at unit boot (credential present) and never resets.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | `subscribeZeropsAgentAuth` + `subscribeServerConfig` over the door WS; 100 ms / 20 ms `ps` samplers on the container      |
| **F1 — upstream's Claude probe is a false positive for logout**: with the credential file absent, `claude auth status` → `{loggedIn:false, authMethod:none}` and `claude -p` → "Not logged in", yet `ClaudeProvider.ts` (~:892-896) derives `auth.status:authenticated "Claude Max Subscription"` from the SDK init message's `subscriptionType`/`tokenSource`, which Claude still reports from `~/.claude.json`'s account. Consequence: `providerAuth` never flips to `unauthenticated` for Claude, the "verify before mark" gate is inert for a planted/stale file, the picker never shows Claude logged out. Fix: the owned feed verifies with `claude auth status` (JSON `loggedIn`) / `codex login status` itself, not the provider probe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | forced `server.refreshProviders` with the file aside; pid-level timing of the probe                                       |
| **F3** — the "targeted refresh" serves `ClaudeDriver`'s capabilities cache (`CAPABILITIES_PROBE_TTL` 5 min): inside the TTL a refresh returns in ~220 ms with no `claude` spawn; a real probe (`claude --version` + a stream-json init spawn, ~3.5–4 s) only when expired. **F4** — each real probe writes `~/.claude/backups` and `~/.claude/sessions`, which the dir-level watcher sees as a second event (one echo re-check ~4 s later, from cache; not a loop). **F5** — the success path (watcher → refresh → spawn) logs nothing; `journalctl -u zerops@z3` stayed empty for the whole session.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | same run                                                                                                                  |
| **F6 — the container-served client was not a hosted-static build**: `z3-dev-push.sh` built the web with `VITE_BASE_PATH` only, so `isHostedStaticApp()` was false and `/z3/` redirected to `/pair`; a seeded session and a door-minted credential pasted into the pair form both fail (`browser_session_unsupported`, HTTP 403 at `auth/http.ts:234`); `/z3/zerops` signs in but lists `z3-eval` as "Connected" with no button (`useConnectedZeropsOrigins` counts the primary origin regardless of auth). The push script now sets `VITE_HOSTED_APP_CHANNEL=container`. Workaround used for the run: `VITE_HOSTED_APP_CHANNEL=latest vp dev` on the laptop → landing → picker → Connect (door from `localhost`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | bundle grep (`VITE_HOSTED_APP_CHANNEL:""`), `_chat.tsx:190` redirect                                                      |
| **Card + terminal move work**: in a draft thread the card lists `claude-code` (reconnect) and `codex` (not-authorized) with Sign-in buttons; "Sign in to Codex" opens the terminal and runs `codex login --device-auth` — output: `https://auth.openai.com/codex/device` (rendered in link colour) + a one-time code; synthetic Ctrl+C on `textarea.t3-ghostty-input` cancels. **F8 — `claude /login` first shows an interactive menu** (1. Claude account with subscription / 2. Console / 3. 3rd-party; Esc to cancel); only after Enter does it print "Browser didn't open? Use the url below to sign in (c to copy)" and a ~330-char `https://claude.com/cai/oauth/authorize?…` URL **soft-wrapped over 4 rows as dim plain text — NOT link-coloured by Ghostty** (no OSC 8 seen); synthetic Esc did not cancel, SIGINT did. A copy-URL affordance is needed for Claude on the web too, not only on the phone. **F7** — with the terminal pane open the card overlaps the empty-state heading (cosmetic). | puppeteer on the dev client + pid sampling on the container; screenshots in the puppeteer store `s7-08`, `s7-11`, `s7-12` |

Lease exclusivity is proven at the state-transition level with fakes, not under two racing connections.                                                                                                                                                                        | `ApnsDeliveryJobStore.ts`, `ApnsDeliveryWorker.ts` + tests (`TestClock` for recovery/expiry) |

### S7 follow-up — self-verified agent auth + server-driven login sessions (laptop, 2026-08-29 afternoon)

| Fact | How it was established |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------- | ------------- | --------- | ------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **The agent-auth feed now verifies itself**: `ZeropsAgentAuthVerify.ts` runs `claude auth status` (JSON `loggedIn`) / `codex login status` (text) through an argv spawner; any spawn failure collapses to `unknown`; `mark-oauth` is gated on this verification, the provider registry refresh is only a best-effort picker-cache warm. `markedOAuth` resets when `ZCP_AGENT_OAUTH_<S>` disappears from the env store; the watcher targets the credential FILE (parent dir filtered by basename) so a probe's writes to `~/.claude/backups`/`sessions` no longer echo; verification results and `mark-oauth` spawns (`changed`, `migrated`) are logged. `ZeropsAgentAuth.ts` reaches the provider registry only through `spi/providerInstances.ts` — zone rule 2 green again. | `ZeropsAgentAuthVerify.test.ts` (21), `ZeropsAgentAuthIo.test.ts`, `ZeropsAgentAuthWatcher.test.ts`, `scripts/z3-zone-architecture.test.ts` 4/4 |
| **Login is server-driven**: `zerops.agentLogin.start {agentId, threadId}` opens an `agent-login-<agent>` terminal, writes the command, feeds the PTY bytes to a near-verbatim port of the Zerops GUI walker's pure output parser (`zeropsAgentLoginOutputParser.ts`: chunk-boundary-safe URL anchors, OSC 8, DEC graphics; 16 ported tests) and handler table trimmed to claude-code/codex, with the walker's stall timer pressing Enter through any unrecognized screen (Claude's login-method menu included); the feed's `login` field carries `phase` (`starting                                                                                                                                                                                                           | menu                                                                                                                                            | awaiting-browser | awaiting-code | succeeded | failed | cancelled`), `url`, `code`, `message`, `terminalId`; success triggers `ZeropsAgentAuth.recheckNow`; one session per agent; `zerops.agentLogin.cancel` sends Ctrl-C and closes. The card renders "Open sign-in link" + "Copy link" (+ "Copy code" for Codex) and no longer types into a client terminal. The raw PTY stream carries no newline at the soft-wrap point — the wrapping seen in F8 was the client terminal's rendering. | `ZeropsAgentLogin.test.ts` (9), `zeropsAgentLoginWalker.test.ts` (17) fixtured on the F8 ledger lines; web card tests |
| Not proven offline: Codex's exact success/error lines beyond `Logged in using <method>` / `Not logged in` (ported from the GUI walker), which stream Codex writes to (both are read), and the Claude menu text (the walker never matches it — it Enters on stall). The `ws.ts` feed handler re-zips two services with `Stream.merge` + re-read instead of `zipLatestWith` to stay clear of this Effect build's debounce/interrupt scheduler bug. | code + the S7 fix report |
