| Claim                                                                                                                                 | Measured                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| A token granted a project seconds after its creation answers at once                                                                  | Both tokens' `/user/info` answered `200` one second after the POST, the project still `CREATING`/`NEW`. One earlier run had the broker-shaped token answer `401` on every call for 90 s and then the project was deleted; it did not recur in three later runs and is unexplained.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| The broker's shape lists the project's services and writes plain and sensitive variables while the service is `NEW`/`READY_TO_DEPLOY` | `POST /service-stack/search` by `projectId` → the `zcp` service at `READY_TO_DEPLOY`; `POST /service-stack/{id}/user-data` ×3 (`GITEA_URL`, `MATE_BROKER_URL` plain, `GITEA_TOKEN` sensitive) all `200`, four seconds after creation; `GET …/user-data` as the same token listed the three with the sensitive content in clear.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| A Mate's shape reads its own service's variables in clear                                                                             | The `NO_ACCESS` + `BASIC_USER` token's `GET /service-stack/{id}/user-data` listed the three, `sensitive: true` on the token and its content in clear.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Variables written before the first boot are there after it                                                                            | The service went `ACTIVE` 80 s after creation; the three were present unchanged. (zcp reads them from the container's live env store, rewritten within seconds of a write — `internal/ops/gitea_repo.go`, measured 2026-09-16 — so no restart is needed for a write before or after boot.)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| A restart before the first deploy is refused, cleanly                                                                                 | `PUT /service-stack/{id}/restart` at `READY_TO_DEPLOY` → `400 serviceStackIsReadyToDeploy` in under a second. At `ACTIVE` → `200` with a process.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Writes during a running restart are accepted and applied after it                                                                     | With `stack.restart` RUNNING: `POST …/user-data` (a new key) `200`, `PUT /user-data/{id}` `{key, content}` (an update) `200`; both listed as `stack.updateUserData` PENDING behind the restart and applied once it finished (new key present, `GITEA_URL` updated). `PUT /user-data/{id}` needs the key beside the content (as measured 2026-09-16 for project env).                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `project.create` fails at random, and its verdict can take half a minute to appear                                                    | Of five creations with the client's YAML today, two got `project.create` FAILED (`internalServerError`, processes `j2cJQm8VSTSyMQEZvm4e9g` 20:21Z on 09-16, `AEdMBXBVTX6wlF2n3R56DQ` 06:49Z on 09-17) with `stack.build` FAILED alongside (`l5mjyAAIRfiGsiHlLX1t9A`, `O9GyHkyvTkqa6oLSRNrUhQ`), the project left `NEW`, the service `READY_TO_DEPLOY`. On the success the terminal `project.create` appeared in `POST /process/search` about 30 s after the POST. A creation must read the verdict and retry on FAILED (the probes now delete and recreate, up to three times). |

## A signer record written after the login lands is not seen until something else changes — 2026-09-16

Test org `Mate`, third run of the released flow (mate 0.11.3): Gitea and the Mate came up, the door admitted the owner, the conversation opened, Claude Code was signed in through Mate.

| Claim                                                                                                                | Measured                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| The app writes `mate:signer:{agent}:{userId}` after the login succeeds, and the server has already published by then | `GET /project/{id}` showed `mate:signer:claude-code:IwbxLTjISnOGy3R7Nj28Bg` (the owner's `/user/info` `id`), while the Mate's UI kept saying the sign-in was not recorded. The server rebuilds its agent snapshot on the credential-file event and reads signers then (30 s cache); the app's tag write follows the `succeeded` phase, and no later event republishes. The dispatch gate reads signers live, so a turn would pass; the composer is replaced by the notice, so none is tried. Read afterwards in code: the snapshot the RPC feed serves (`latest`) was built without authorizers at all, so no client could ever see a record, republish or not, and signing in again would not have helped. Fixed for 0.11.4: `latest` is the last published snapshot, the server rechecks signers every 35 s while a credentialed agent has no recorded signer, and the client shows the record it just wrote. |

## A project creation that the platform failed after answering 200 — 2026-09-16

Test org `Mate`, second run of the released flow from an emptied org: the Gitea project and the Mate project created one second apart by the client.

| Claim                                                                                               | Measured                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `POST /client/{id}/project` can answer 200 with a project whose `project.create` process then fails | Gitea `goAPyoSmR2u4U65zehEjig` created 20:21:16Z, every service built; the Mate project `txRlx5AcRbexBQEkAUIDLg` at 20:21:17Z got `project.create` FAILED (`internalServerError`, process `j2cJQm8VSTSyMQEZvm4e9g`, 20:21:18.151Z) and `stack.build` FAILED (`l5mjyAAIRfiGsiHlLX1t9A`, `pipelineFailed` 20:21:19Z). The project stayed `NEW`, its `zcp` service `READY_TO_DEPLOY`, `GET /project/{id}/log` answered no url. The first run (19:2xZ, same timing) had succeeded. The client never read the process and showed "Coming up" indefinitely.       |
| `POST /process/search` lists an org's processes with their verdicts                                 | Body `{search: [{name: "clientId", operator: "eq", value}], sort: [{name: "created", ascending: false}], limit}` → `items[]` with `id`, `actionName` (`project.create`, `stack.create`, `stack.build`, `stack.enableSubdomainAccess`, `stack.updateUserData`, `stack.restart`, `project.delete`, `zL7Master.instanceL7BalancerConfigUpdate`), `status` (`FINISHED`, `FAILED`, `CANCELED`), `created`, `projectId`, `error` (`{code, message, meta}` or null). A `projectId` filter narrows it to one project. Processes of a deleted project remain listed. |

## A real Mate through the backbone: its repository, its branch, its recipe, a stage from what it pushed — 2026-09-16

Onboarding org, a Mate made by the platform's development-container recipe (`PUT /project/{pid}/first-class-recipe/development-container` with a `zcp@1` service, `startWithoutCode`), the `feat/auth-backbone` zcp delivered into it as the e2e test binary by `zcli push`, the Gitea project and broker from `gitea-mate` beside it; everything tagged `probe:realmate-2026-09-16` and deleted, counts equal (9 tokens, 10 projects). Scripts `probe_realmate_*`. Four fixes landed first (zcp `9ff83cc6`, `9cc02f5c`, `823f769c`; gitea-mate `3fea2f2`).

| Claim                                                                                    | Measured                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| The three Gitea variables written by the app reach zcp's process after a restart         | `POST /service-stack/{zcp}/user-data` ×3 (`GITEA_URL`, `MATE_BROKER_URL` plain, `GITEA_TOKEN` sensitive) then `PUT /service-stack/{id}/restart`; inside the container zcp logged the wiring ready with none missing.                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| A dev pair gets its repository from the broker and the Mate pushes its branch            | `POST /mate/repository` `200` in 0.59 s; zcp reported `rm/rm1312dev` wired and the pair on `mate/{bot}`; `zerops_deploy strategy="git-push"` → `PUSHED`, the branch with `index.js` and `zerops.yaml` on Gitea. Only once the Gitea reconcile ran after the metas were complete: at `complete step=provision` the metas are partial and every pass skipped the pair, and the terminal step's message builder replaced the reconcile lines. |
| The Mate proposes the group recipe as a pull request from its fork                       | `zerops_workflow action="group-recipe"` → PR #1 on `rm/group` with `0 — AI Agent`, `3 — Stage`, `4 — Small Production` and the root README; a second call answers "already current". A pair that never deployed needs `action="set-default-setup"` first (`SetupName required`).                                                                                                                                                           |
| Gitea answers `400 "sha not found [<ref>]"` for a tree read of a ref that does not exist | `GET /repos/{o}/{r}/git/trees/{ref}` on a missing branch is `400`, not `404`; a "is this branch new" check must accept both.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| A Mate's fresh history does not merge into the seeded `main`                             | the broker's repository is born with an initial commit; a branch from zcp's own `git init` shares no history: `merge` and `squash` refuse ("The merge head and base do not share a common history"), `rebase` succeeds. `POST /repos/{o}/{r}/pulls/{n}/merge` is `405 "User not allowed to merge PR"` to the site admin when a merge whitelist names a team he is not in.                                                                  |
| A tier's `import.yaml` cannot be imported as composed                                    | `buildFromGit` on a private Gitea repository → `400 projectImportInvalidParameter {"…buildFromGit":["public git repository doesn't exist"]}`; without `buildFromGit`, `zeropsSetup` → `["parameter is required for use of pipelineConfig"]`. The app's converter (`startWithoutCode: true`, the source map kept) is the only way in; the broker deploys into the empty service.                                                            |
| The broker deploys what the Mate pushed, and says why it cannot                          | after the code and `environments.yaml` merged: `appVersionName` = the merge sha 11 s later, service `ACTIVE`, status `mate/deploy/stage/rm1312` `pending` → deploying; a head without `zerops.yaml` is refused with the reason in the broker's log, not deployed.                                                                                                                                                                          |
| A `${service_var}` reference can reach a container before it resolves                    | `web` booted with `OIDC_CLIENT_SECRET` holding the literal `${broker_OIDC_CLIENT_SECRET}` (28 characters, so an emptiness guard passed it); `admin auth add-oauth` wrote an unusable source and every callback failed `invalid_client` until the recipe learned to treat that shape as not arrived and to refresh an existing source.                                                                                                      |
| The eval host is gone                                                                    | `eval-zcp` (`waAzEFn6SBaysG4YE4rv7A`) → `400 projectNotFound`; no eval-shaped `zcp` service in any of the 17 orgs; SSH to a container from this machine needs `zcli vpn up`, which needs `sudo`. `make e2e-zcp` and `build-deploy.sh` are unusable until a new eval project exists.                                                                                                                                                        |

## A person's Gitea token from the broker: the Gitea facts, measured on the lab — 2026-09-17

The local lab Gitea 1.27.2 (`scratchpad/gitea-lab`, OAuth2 source `zerops-idp` id 1), the site admin's CLI-minted token and basic auth; probe users and tokens deleted after. What D21 rests on: the broker, as site admin, makes a person's account and mints them a token, so the app never sends a person through Gitea's pages.

| Claim                                                                                    | Measured                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| An account with no login source needs a password; one bound to an OAuth2 source does not | `POST /admin/users {username, email, must_change_password: false}` → `400 PasswordIsRequired`; the same with `source_id: 1, login_name: probe-sub-1` → `201`, the account `active`, `login_name` and `source_id` read back through `GET /admin/users`.                                                    |
| The site admin's basic auth mints a token for a person, and the token acts as them       | `POST /users/u-probe1/tokens {name: mate-app/1, scopes: [read:user, read:organization, read:repository, write:repository, write:issue, read:package]}` with basic auth → `201` with the value; `GET /user` with it → `u-probe1`; `GET /user/orgs` → `200 []`; a repository the person cannot see → `404`. |
| A token name a user already holds is refused; a token deletes by name                    | the same name again → `400 "access token name has been used already"`; `DELETE /users/u-probe1/tokens/mate-app%2F1` → `204`. Hence `mate-app/{unix nanoseconds}`.                                                                                                                                         |
| The CLI's `change-password` flags the admin as must-change                               | `gitea admin user change-password` without `--must-change-password=false` made every API call answer `403 "You must change your password"`.                                                                                                                                                               |

## Gitea answers every origin: `[cors] ALLOW_DOMAIN = *`, measured on the lab — 2026-09-17

The same lab Gitea 1.27.2 with `[cors] ENABLED = true`, `ALLOW_DOMAIN = *`, `ALLOW_CREDENTIALS = false`, `HEADERS = Content-Type,User-Agent,Authorization`, restarted; curl with `Origin` headers. What D22 rests on: once every browser call carries a bearer and no cookie (D21), an origin allowlist protects nothing and only pins a Gitea to the origin that made it.

| Claim                                                                        | Measured                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| The preflight from a dev origin is answered `*` with `Authorization` allowed | `OPTIONS /api/v1/user` with `Origin: http://localhost:5734`, `Access-Control-Request-Method: GET`, `Access-Control-Request-Headers: authorization` → `200`, `Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *`, `Access-Control-Allow-Headers: Authorization`, `Access-Control-Allow-Methods: GET`, `Access-Control-Max-Age: 600`. |
| A token call from any origin carries `*`; the token's scopes still decide    | `GET /api/v1/user` with a `write:repository` token and `Origin: https://elsewhere.example` → `403` (the scope: `/user` needs `read:user`) with `Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *`; the same from `http://localhost:5734` → the same.                                                                               |
| A call with no token is refused from any origin                              | `GET /api/v1/user` with `Origin: https://elsewhere.example` and no token → `401`, `Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *`. The header lets a browser read the refusal; it grants nothing.                                                                                                                               |

A `502` from a service on `*.zerops.app` carries none of the service's words; the app read it as "Gitea is still setting up" and retried silently. Fixed: a Gitea refusal is `424 gitea_refused` with Gitea's words (v3.2), shown in place of the sign-in line (mate 0.11.8).                                                                         |

The test org's broker (`b1xAVGtKRiG01QQD3dhlwA`) redeployed from gitea-mate `main` with `zcli push -P <gitea project> -S <broker> --setup broker --working-dir ~/www/gitea-mate` under an isolated `HOME` and a temporary `NO_ACCESS` token holding `BASIC_USER` on the Gitea project (deleted after): build and deploy in 131 s, the service `ACTIVE` throughout, the new broker listening 20 s after "Application is deploying". | Claim                                                                                    | Measured                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Gitea 1.27.2 answers a duplicate branch rule `403 "Branch protection already exist"`** | the v3.3 broker's `set_branch_rule … rule=main` → `403 on POST /repos/imperial-titan/group/branch_protections: Branch protection already exist`; the apply path waited for a 422 before editing, so `main` kept `enable_merge_whitelist: true, merge_whitelist_teams: [release], block_admin_merge_override: true` through every pass. Fixed in v3.4: the rule is listed and edited when it exists.                                                                                                                                                                                         |

The whitelist has to go first; the plan orders the rule before the merge — and the merge is still refused in the pass that edited the rule (v3.4's first pass, 12:35:21Z: `applied 1, failures 1`) and lands on the next (12:38:21Z, `merged_by: admin`; `main` then carries `0 — AI Agent/`, `3 — Stage/`, `4 — Small Production/`). A fresh org never meets this: its rule exists before any pull request. |

## A refresh that re-took its leases painted "Reading your projects…" over the list it had — 2026-09-17

The owner's screenshots at about 14:50Z on the localhost pair (mate 0.11.10), waiting for a Mate to come up: the projects page at "Reading your projects…" with an empty left menu, then the roster again ("every now and then when waiting for the mate to come up it does this full refresh, that's crazy bad"). The cause read from the code; the fix measured by the runtime's own test, in the browser only at the owner's next creation.

| Claim                                                                      | Measured                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **A refresh re-took every lease, and a released lease drops what it read** | `refreshZeropsCandidates` bumped a version that re-keyed each `InterestDemand`'s lease. The last lease of an interest releases the organization's queries (`releaseLease` → `releaseInactiveQueries` deletes them) and closes its receiver, so `projectsOf` and `servicesOf` read empty until the new lease's baseline landed; the page keyed its "Reading your projects…" line, its empty-state invitation and the menu's tree on `isLoading && candidates.length === 0`. Every trigger blanked the same way: the 0.11.9 creation clock (every 20 s while a creation is on its way — the owner's case), the header's reload, the access renewal at 14 min, the refresh after every start, restart and connect. |
| **A refresh on a fresh receiver keeps what the reads hold**                | `runtime.refresh(organization)` (0.11.11) re-establishes every leased interest of the organization on a new receiver — the resume-from-background path without the pause — and releases nothing. `runtime.test.ts` "re-reads an organization's inventory on a fresh receiver and keeps what it holds": across the refresh no published state has the organization's projects empty or its query back to `unresolved`; the baseline is read again (a project renamed meanwhile reads renamed after); one receiver closed and one opened; an organization nobody holds opens nothing.                                                                                                                             |
| **The painters key on "read once", not on "a read in flight"**             | `useZeropsCandidates.readOnce` is the organization's projects query being `observed`, which a refresh keeps; the "Reading…" line, `hasNoZeropsProject` and the menu's tree take `unread = !readOnce`, and the header's reload glyph alone spins on `isLoading`. `ZeropsProjectsPage.test.ts` "keeps an empty organization's invitation up while its list is re-read".                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

## The owner's second run on 0.11.11: the recipe merged by the broker in nine seconds — 2026-09-17

The test org `Mate`, emptied at 14:30Z the day before this section's clock (all times UTC), the owner's own _New project_ through the localhost pair at 12:46Z on the 0.11.10 client (0.11.11 from 13:08Z), gitea-mate `main` at `1127415`. Read as the owner through the API and, for Gitea, with the admin token read through a temporary integration token deleted at once; a watcher sampled the org's projects and the group repo every 45 s.

| Claim                                                                                                    | Measured                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Gitea and the Mate came up, and the loop delivered, as on the earlier runs                               | `Imperial Titan - dev` (bot Dara) created 12:46:19Z, its `zcp` ACTIVE with `initComplete` at 12:48:06Z; Gitea's `web`, `broker`, `db`, `volume` ACTIVE, the registry tagged (`mate:gn`, `mate:gm … :mate`), the signer tag on the Mate; `GITEA_URL`, `MATE_BROKER_URL`, `GITEA_TOKEN` on the Mate's `zcp`; `ZCP_API_KEY` not on the container (open item 1, as on Zane).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| **The first prompt made one simple-mode service, no pair, and the Git tab said "no repository yet"**     | "create a todo app" at 13:16:59Z (Claude Code, `bypassPermissions`): no recipe matched, so zcp's classic route; the agent's plan chose `bootstrapMode: simple` — `tododb` (postgresql 18) and `todoapp` (nodejs 22) created 13:17:39Z, a self-deploy of `todoapp` built 13:19:01Z–13:19:57Z and verified, the app live. The Mate had mounted `todoapp:/var/www` over sshfs at 13:18:14Z; the mount dropped at 13:19:46Z when the deploy replaced the container ("remote host has disconnected"). The Git tab lists the mounted runtimes and asks git on the service, so the row read `todoapp · no repository yet`: a simple-mode service deploys over its own working copy and keeps no checkout. zcp's own gate says the same (`launch_source_control_gate.go`: a service in mode simple "cannot act as a push source — production promotion needs a repo-backed pair"). Only `bootstrapMode: standard` makes a dev/stage pair; the owner's prompt named none, and the agent chose. |
| **The broker made the service repository on the deploy (slice 2.1), empty**                              | `imperial-titan/todoapp` created 13:18:17Z through `POST /mate/repository` — "A service of imperial-titan", `main` with the initial commit by `admin`, Dara's bot `mate-{projectId}` the one collaborator, no other branch, no pull request: nothing was pushed, since a direct deploy pushes nothing (as on Juno's run).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| **A Mate's recipe pull request merges by itself (D23), on a fresh org**                                  | Dara's PR #1 "Mate: the group's import files" from `mate/mate-{projectId}` against `main`: opened 13:18:23Z, merged 13:18:32Z by `admin` (the broker's token; the `pull_request` hook nudged the loop). `main` after: `0 — AI Agent/import.yaml` and `README.md`, `3 — Stage`, `4 — Small Production`, the README — on top of the initial commit of 12:52:03Z. The service repo `imperial-titan/todoapp` existed by then.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| **The Git tab's row stayed at "no repository yet" after Dara made the checkout; a reload showed it**     | The row (`CheckoutProbe`) subscribes to the server's `vcs.status` for `/var/www/todoapp`, loaded once at subscription; the turn's end refreshed the thread's cwd only — `/var/www` (the Mate's log: `cwd: '/var/www'`), never a repository — and the 30 s poller refreshes remote refs alone (`VcsStatusBroadcaster`). 0.11.12: `CheckpointReactor` refreshes every mounted checkout it resolves for the turn; "refreshes every mounted checkout on turn completion, not only the workspace root". Reaches a Mate at its next update; a change made outside a turn still needs the reload.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| **An expansion into a pair keeps the dev half's hostname, so the pair reads `todoapp` / `todoappstage`** | zcp `bootstrap_outputs.go`: "Mode-expansion path: dev/simple → standard with IsExisting=true", the service meta merged; the agent's own gate names it (`route=adopt`, `bootstrapMode: standard`, an explicit `stageHostname`). The fork folded a stage under `{name}dev` only (0.11.10), so `todoappstage` would have stood alone in the map and in the Git tab; 0.11.12 folds it under `{name}` as well (`devPartnerHostname`) — "folds a stage under the single service it was expanded from", "lists a service expanded from a single one as the dev half, never its stage". Unmeasured live until the expansion runs.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

## The owner's own poking after the run: Gitea's sign-in 500, "Checking" on an approved release, 700 reads a minute — 2026-09-17

The owner opened the Git tab in their browser (19:35Z on) and followed a pull request's link to Gitea's pages. Read from inside: Gitea's (`web`) and the broker's runtime logs, the statuses as Gitea holds them, the code.

| Claim                                                                                 | Measured                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Gitea's own _Sign in with Zerops_ answers 500 for a person the broker made (Q-17)** | The broker's OIDC leg went through (19:41:22Z `/oidc/authorize` 302, `/oidc/complete` 200, `/oidc/token` 200, `/oidc/userinfo` 200); Gitea: `createUserInContext() [E] CreateUser: user already exists [name: u-iwbxltjisnogy3r7nj28bg]`, the callback 500 in 41 ms. Gitea's callback looks for an external-login row, never for a user bound by `login_name`, and `[oauth2_client] ACCOUNT_LINKING = disabled` (written before the broker made accounts) refuses to link. Fixed: `auto` links the arriving `u-…` identity to the account of that username (gitea-mate v3.7 `ad04611`); the test org's `web` redeployed with it. |
| **The Git tab said "Checking" on `v0.1.0`, approved and deployed an hour before**     | Gitea holds one status on the tagged commit: `mate/release/v0.1.0 success approved: u-iwbx…` (19:20:55Z). The client read a status's `state`; Gitea's JSON carries it under `status`, so no state ever read and every release was "pending" — "Checking" — and every check on a branch none. Fixed (fork `6790efee0`): the client maps `status` onto `state`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| **The environment rows' deploy statuses were read from the wrong repository**         | `GET /api/v1/repos/todo/app/commits/0db51c04…/statuses` → 404, on repeat: the read named the runtime (`app`) while the pair's repository is `appdev` (the tier's `buildFromGit`). Gitea holds on `todo/appdev@0db51c04`: `mate/deploy/todo-production/app` pending 19:20:55Z → success 19:21:59Z, `mate/deploy/todo-stage/app` pending 18:51:51Z → success 18:52:45Z, and the workflow's `Zerops deploy / deploy (push)` failure "Failing after 3s" 18:52:00Z. Fixed (fork `2c9f0bc79`): the read goes to the repository the tier names, keyed by the hostname.                                                                  |
| **The open Git tab read the group repo ~700 times a minute**                          | Gitea's API log: 17–31 GETs a minute until 19:34, then 712, 723, 740, 631, 673, 710, 754, 726, 734 a minute from 19:35Z — `environments.yaml`, the open pull requests, the two tier files and the statuses, ~1 570 each in nine minutes. `useZeropsGroupDeploys`' effect was keyed on the groups' identity and the reader, which the Git tab rebuilds from an inventory that moves with every read the hook makes. Fixed (fork `2c9f0bc79`): the reads run off the serialised key and the minute clock; the rate fell to 20–170 a minute within minutes of the change reaching the tab.                                          |
| **The empty recipe request closed by the broker**                                     | The test org's broker redeployed from `main` (v3.7, 144 s, `zcli push … --setup broker`); `todo/group` PR #6 `closed`, not merged, within its next pass.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| **`zcli push` names the setups of `zerops.yaml`, not the services**                   | `--setup web` is refused: "available setups: gitea, broker, runner" — Gitea's `web` service deploys with `--setup gitea`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| **The person's own-page sign-in works once `web` runs v3.7 (Q-17 closed)**            | `web` redeployed in place (`zcli push … -S web --setup gitea`, 113 s, Gitea answering throughout the switch); in the audit browser, signed in to the app: Gitea's _Sign In_ → _Sign in with Zerops_ → the dashboard as `u-iwbxltjisnogy3r7nj28bg` ("Ales Rechtorik - Dashboard", the `todo` org's activity listed), no consent step shown — the app's session completed it. The account is the one the broker made, linked, not a second one.                                                                                                                                                                                    |

Driven through the UI in the audit browser as the test owner, on the org `Mate`, against the dev pair.
Two shapes in one sitting: the existing **Todo** (two Mates, a stage and a production) and a
**Notes** project made from nothing with one Mate and one production and no stage.

| Claim                                                                       | Measured                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **The page mints an environment's deploy token, as the person**             | Opening `/zerops` as the owner minted `deploy-Todo - production` then `deploy-Todo - stage` (`NO_ACCESS`, `BASIC_USER` on that project) and wrote each as a secret variable on the broker's service. _Add production_ on Notes minted `deploy-Notes - production` during the creation itself.                                                                                                                  |
| **The platform takes the 62-character variable name**                       | `MATE_DEPLOY_TOKEN_375446707654687352466D42643867566E4E48504A67` (Todo - stage), `…794F727A64654E59527A57384D77476F4B3742467841` (Todo - production), `…505A42396947454E523665554C707967305451423441` (Notes - production) — all three written and read back by name.                                                                                                                                          |
| **A deleted runner is re-imported and takes the job**                       | `runnertodo` had been deleted the day before. The merge at 10:19:24Z queued a job; the service was created at 10:19:25Z and the job started at 10:20:55Z — about 90 s from merge to running. A second org got its own: `runnernotes`, for the group `notes`.                                                                                                                                                   |
| **A push to `main` deploys the stage, by `zcli push`, on the job**          | `todo/appdev` run 5, push of the merge of pull request #4: status `pending dispatched` → `pending deploying · job 5` at 10:23:12Z → `success live` at 10:24:08Z. 58 s from the grant to live.                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| **`actions/checkout` takes a bare sha from Gitea**                          | `git checkout --progress --force 5ebebe2c4f69f93621c2e8503952e5f358d207ef` in the job's log — the `ref: inputs.sha \|\| github.sha` the workflow passes.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| **`zcli push` takes its key from the environment, on a throwaway `HOME`**   | `Using config file: /tmp/tmp.fTYLgARVw8/.config/zerops/.zcli.yml`; the grant printed as `deploy: app of todo-stage ← 5ebebe2c…` and the token only ever as `JOB_TOKEN: ***`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| **The build runs on Zerops, not on the runner**                             | The job's log carries the platform's own build: `✅ npm install --omit=dev`, `Application deploy artefact size 2.7 MiB`, `Application artefact uploaded in 0s`, `➤ INFO Application is deploying`.                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| **A push whose branch feeds no environment ends green**                     | `notes/appdev` run 6, the push of Iris's merge with no environment declared yet: `success` in 5 s, the grant answering `nothing`. This is the red check of primer open 21, gone.                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| **A release is dispatched, and only a release reaches a production**        | Notes: `v0.1.0` tagged from the row → `mate/release/v0.1.0 success approved` 10:39:44Z → `mate/deploy/notes-production/app pending dispatched` 10:39:45Z → `deploying · job 7` 10:39:50Z → `success live` 10:42:08Z. 2 min 24 s from tag to live; the run's event is `workflow_dispatch` on `main`.                                                                                                            |
| **A project with no stage releases what is merged (D28)**                   | Notes declares one production and no stage. _Release_ was offered, and the tag listed `app 729eb446` — the head of `notes/appdev`'s `main`, not a deployed commit. Before the change the offer read "The stage has not deployed anything to release." for ever.                                                                                                                                                |
| **A fresh repository carries the current workflow; an old one never would** | `notes/appdev`, wired by zcp v9.180.0, had `zeropsio/gitea-mate/actions/deploy@v4` with `workflow_dispatch` on its first commit. `todo/appdev` still had `@v1` on `main` and on both open pull requests, because zcp writes the file only in the pass that first wires a repository and a wired pair never reaches that pass again — put there by hand for this run, and fixed in zcp so a delivery brings it. |
| **zcp titles a pull request after the task, live**                          | Iris opened `notes/appdev` #1 as "Build a small notes app: list notes (title+body), add a note, delete a note. …" — v9.179.3's title, on its first delivery.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| **A second Mate's pull request dies when the first lands**                  | `todo/appdev` #3 (Fen) went `mergeable: false` the moment #4 (Vera) merged, and the row simply lost its _Merge_ — nothing said why. A branch is cut from `main` when the repository is wired and nothing ever caught it up. Fixed: a delivery merges the base in first, and a collision it cannot settle is named.                                                                                             |
| **Three list endpoints answer under a key the client did not read**         | `GET /project/{id}/service-stack` under `list` (not `items`), `GET /client/{id}/user/list` under `clientUserList`, and the project page's count as `total` (not `totalCount`). Each read empty and said nothing: the page could not find the broker, so no deploy token was ever minted. `GET /service-stack/{id}/env` does use `items` and is not paged; `/user-data` pages at 20 by default.                 |
| **A repair the effect is torn down before reaching is never retried**       | With both of Todo's environments missing a key, one reload minted one. The list marked every entry attempted before the work, so the abort of the first effect left the rest permanently skipped in that tab.                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

### What the owner found watching it, the same afternoon

| Claim                                                                  | Measured                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **_Release_ looked inert while the stage was deploying**               | The tag listed what the stage was running, so pressing it during a stage deploy produced a tag nobody could see move. `v0.1.1` was in fact created, listing `app 9076c981`. A release now lists what is merged, whatever a stage is doing.                                                                                                                                                                      |
| **A failed merge said nothing a person could act on**                  | "Gitea would not merge it: Gitea refused to merge the pull request." — the client kept Gitea's status and threw its `message` away, and the conversation showed neither. Gitea's own sentence is now in the message, and the Mate's conversation is where it lands.                                                                                                                                             |
| **Merging from the conversation works; the checkout never followed**   | The row above the composer read "Fen is waiting on you to merge #6."; pressing _Merge_ squashed it onto `main` (`5686decd`). This row first said the Mate's checkout was then pulled: it was not. The pull had no working directory, and one with none is refused before it starts (`resolveScope`, read after the last tests below); the pull is removed, and the branch takes `main` in at its next delivery. |
| **A release's contents are one line per task**                         | Todo's release row read `5686dec  Mate: appdev (#6)` — Gitea's squash-merge subject, which is the pull request's title and number. The read is `GET /repos/{o}/{r}/compare/{deployed}...{main}`, and `404` for a pair it cannot compare is an empty list rather than a failure.                                                                                                                                 |
| **An agent can resolve a conflict in its own checkout, asked plainly** | Fen was told "run git fetch origin and git merge origin/main, resolve any conflict so both changes survive, then deploy appstage again". It merged, resolved `index.html`, redeployed, and #6 became mergeable — the sequence the delivery now performs by itself.                                                                                                                                              |
| **A wired pair's git-push deploy aimed at protected `main`**           | Vera's `zerops_deploy` on `appdev` with `git-push` failed seven times: "rejected as non-fast-forward … a pushed branch tip is behind its remote counterpart". The pair carried `main` as its tracked ref from before it had a branch, and the tracked ref was resolved before the Mate's own. The agent pushed to its branch by hand each time. Fixed in zcp v9.180.2; the Mates were restarted onto it.        |

## The last tests before the hand-off to zcp hardening: merge, release, roll back, a second task — 2026-09-18

Driven through the UI as the test owner, zcp v9.180.2 on all three Mates. The first three legs ran
on the dev pair (localhost:5734). It went down mid-run, when an upstream port landed in the same
tree: the server could not find `@effect/platform-node`, and the client threw
`SchemaTransformation.transformEffect is not a function`. So the rollback and the Notes legs ran on
mate.zerops.io, the same client, signed in as the test owner in a fresh browser. It connected to
Iris by itself.

| Claim                                                                       | Measured                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **A merge from the conversation squashes, and the stage follows**           | Vera's `todo/appdev` #7 was merged from its banner at 12:13:26Z and landed as `5b2f7d4 "Show total todo count next to open count, e.g. "3 of 7 open" (#7)"`. Push run 13 went `deploying · job 13` at 12:13:34Z and `live` at 12:14:31Z: 63 s from the merge. The stage serves `` `${open} of ${todos.length} open` ``. Fen's #8 followed the same way: merged 12:27:43Z, live 12:28:41Z (58 s).                                      |
| **The banner stayed after its request was merged**                          | Seen twice: #7 on a page open for hours, #8 on a page just reloaded. The sidebar dropped the request within a second. The banner kept "Fen is waiting on you to merge #8." with _Merge_, never read "Merging…", and went only when the conversation remounted. Cause: `composerBannerItems` is memoised by hand and its dependencies never named `mateReviewBannerItem`. `react-hooks/exhaustive-deps` is off, so nothing flagged it. |
| **The pull after a merge never ran**                                        | `useVcsPullAction({ cwd: null })`: `resolveScope` answers a missing directory with a failure before any command runs, and the verb swallowed it. The pull is removed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| **A wired pair's git-push goes to the Mate's branch (v9.180.2)**            | Fen's first deploy of the task, a git-push, read "Finished." in 2 s. `mate/mate-XGiEULUtRMagwBWsCAhDog` moved to `187d937`, `main` stayed where it was, and #8 opened at 12:22:20Z, 44 s after the task was sent. No push in the turn was rejected; on v9.180.1 Vera's were rejected seven times.                                                                                                                                     |
| **The delivery takes the base in by itself (v9.180.1)**                     | Iris's branch holds `afed1e5` (the task, 12:23) and then `f4b4f3b "Merge remote-tracking branch 'origin/main' into mate/mate-VF3RrXzBTIi5OGt8sEo88A"` (12:26), both as the bot. That is the delivery's order: commit, fetch, merge, push. Fen had merged `main` by hand before editing ("I merged origin/main in again before making the rename edit"), so its delivery found nothing to take.                                        |
| **A request opened after a merge carries only the new work**                | `notes/appdev` #2: 3 files, +17 −0, after #1 landed as a merge commit. `todo/appdev` #8: 1 file, +3 −3, after #6 landed as a squash. A local trial merge of `main` into both Mate branches of `todo/appdev` was clean and left no diff against `main`: pre-squash commits merge clean against their squash.                                                                                                                           |
| **A request is titled after its task**                                      | Notes #2 "Show a live character count under the body field of the add-note form". Todo #8 "Rename the app to "Team Todo" in the page title and main heading, then deploy." carries the task's deploy instruction in its title.                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| **_Release_ on a project with a stage lists what is merged**                | Todo's row read `release · 5686dec`, `DEPLOYED`, _Release_, with `df1a1bd Rename the app … (#8)` and `5b2f7d4 Show total todo count … (#7)` under it, while the stage ran `main`. | **Notes' second release**                                                   | The row read `release · 729eb44` with `12e57c2 Show a live character count … (#2)` under it. Production serves `<span id="body-count" class="char-count">0 characters</span>`.                                                                                                              |
| **Every stage deploy of a wired pair is recorded dirty**                    | Iris, on its first task: "the deploy to stage was marked `dirty` (uncommitted changes existed alongside the committed HEAD at deploy time)". zcp records `attempt.Dirty` from the tree it deploys and commits that tree afterwards, in the delivery (`deploy_ssh.go`), so fresh work is dirty by construction.                                                                                                                        |

Banner and sidebar both read "Merging…", both went back to _Merge_ for about a second at 13:09:37Z (the verb stops pending before the re-read lands), and both were gone at 13:09:38Z. The stage served the footer at 13:11:37Z.                                                                        |

## What the composer's guard was doing — 2026-09-19

The loop left open above, taken apart. Measured on the dev pair (web 5734) in an audit browser, and
in a component test that drives the real editor.

| Claim                                                           | Measured                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| --------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **A typing burst still wedges a renderer**                      | 78 real keystrokes (`agent-browser batch`, one `press` each) into a Mate thread's composer — plain letters, no mentions, no contexts — left the renderer at **101 % CPU** and the tab unresponsive to CDP. The same thread had wedged once before for another reason (the inventory read window), so the burst is the reproduction and the wedge is not yet pinned to one of the two.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| **The editor's round trip is not the fault by itself**          | The real editor in a controlled parent settles: every prompt handed to it comes back byte-identical (newlines, tabs, emoji, mentions, skills), and a burst of keystrokes costs exactly one report and one commit each — focused or not (`ComposerPromptEditor.controlled.test.tsx`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| **The guard swallowed keystrokes**                              | Lexical commits an update in a microtask, and an update that finds one already pending schedules none — so a keystroke queued before the commit is carried _by_ it. With the flag still raised, the listener dropped that commit whole: the editor held `abc` while the screen was told nothing and stayed on `ab`. Proven by a test that fails on the old code, passes on the new (`0a3a462f9`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| **What the divergence costs**                                   | Screen and editor holding different text is what makes them rewrite each other: the screen writes its value into the editor, the editor reports its own, and each write is a sync-lane store write — which is how `Maximum update depth exceeded` arrives by the fiftieth commit.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| **The fix, and what is not yet measured**                       | The controlled write now reads back what the editor holds and records that, inside the same update; the echo is then silent by comparison rather than by timing. Not re-measured live: the signed-in audit browser was lost to a temporary Chrome profile, and local entry needs a Zerops sign-in.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| **One Mate's update was shown on every Mate**                   | Reported from the running app: pressing _Update_ on one agent put "Updating…" on the top bar of all of them, and then a socket error although the server had updated. The header and the Mate card are each one component reused across Mates (no `key` on `ChatView` per environment), so the hook's `useState` — the phase and the held `checkUpdate` answer — carried from one Mate to the next, and the version the wait watched was whichever Mate the header had moved on to.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| **A restart is what an update looks like**                      | `zerops.mate.update` restarts the server, closing the socket its own answer would come back on; the client called that a failed update. Both closed in `8614d3c55`: the state is keyed by environment, the proof is the descriptor's version for that Mate, and a transport failure keeps waiting instead of turning the line red. Reported live, fixed with tests; not yet re-measured in the app.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| **The meter counted a subagent's window as the conversation's** | Reported from the running app: the composer read `High · 200k` while the meter read `11% · 106k/1m`. A turn's `modelUsage` names every model the turn touched, and the adapter took the **largest** window in it — so one Task on a million-token model redrew a 200k conversation as 1m, and 53% full read as 11%. The window now comes from the model the session runs (the catalog's answer for the 200k/1m choice that decides the model id), then from that model's own entry in the report, and only then from the largest; a mid-session model switch moves it too. Reproduced in `ClaudeAdapter.test.ts` (a 200k session with a 1m subagent), fixed in `dd4ce53bb`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| **That fix reached only the sessions that had chosen**          | The same reading came back after `dd4ce53bb`. The window is read from the selection first, but a session whose `modelSelection` never arrives — or carries another provider instance's id, which the adapter drops whole — has neither a selection nor an api model id, so both of the new authorities were skipped and the old largest-in-`modelUsage` still answered. The SDK documents that map as covering "the main loop, Task subagents, sidechains, and internal calls such as compaction", so its widest entry is never a fact about the conversation. Reaching that last resort means no selection reached the session, and an expanded window is never a default — it has to be asked for — so the **narrowest** entry is the one the conversation can be held to, and understating the room left is the safe way for a gauge to be wrong. Zeroed entries, which a crash result carries, are not windows and are skipped. RED then GREEN in `ClaudeAdapter.test.ts` ("a conversation with no model selection is not measured by the largest model that ran": 1000000 before, 200000 after). |

## The menu read as a list of Mates, not as where work is — 2026-09-19

The owner asked for the foot to carry the account, the four glyphs to fold away, and the whole
project → Mates → pull requests → stage → production layout to be thought through for someone who
does not know what merge, stage or production mean, without taking anything from someone who does.
What follows is what the reading found and what was changed; the layout decisions themselves belong
in `spec-mate.md`, and this table holds only what was measured or proven by a test.
