Whatever produced these files re-emitted them from a parsed document.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| **The composer is `BuildGroupRecipe`, and the fallback is still on zcp's HEAD** | Corrected: the first pass read `internal/authoring/recipe/yaml_emitter.go`, which composes **recipes** and has written `ZeropsSetupProd` since v9.0.0 — the wrong file. A group repo's tiers come from `internal/ops/bundle/group.go`, whose `yaml.Marshal` is exactly the four-space, alphabetical, comment-free shape above. Its line 243 sets the promoted (stage/production) service's setup to `firstNonBlank(r.StageSetupName, r.SetupName)`, and `gitea_recipe_reconcile.go:246` already resolved `SetupName` as `firstNonEmptySetup(m.PrimarySetupName, m.StageSetupName, m.Hostname)`. With no `StageSetupName` recorded, both fall through to the **hostname** — `appdev`. The comment directly above line 243 states the rule the fallback breaks: _"A group environment runs what the pair's stage half runs: the dev half's setup is the dev loop's, never a stage's."_ Present on zcp `d09cba98`. |
| **The platform's reachability check was routed around**                         | `harbor/appdev`'s `zerops.yaml` declared, for setup `appdev`: `ports: [3000, httpSupport: true]`, `start: zsc noop --silent`, and **no `healthCheck`**. An HTTP port with nothing checking it. The `prod` setup beside it did declare one — and nothing referenced `prod`, ever. `spec-workflows.md` states the rule the tier broke: healthCheck is `none (dev)`, `required (stage)`, `required` (production).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| **So "Deployed" was not a missing probe, it was a skipped one**                 | Zerops verifies reachability when a setup asks it to. The dev setup asks for nothing, by design, because a dev container's app is started on demand. Naming that setup on a production hands the platform a deploy with nothing to fail: `zsc noop --silent` starts, the version goes ACTIVE, and the L7 serves its placeholder to the only port anybody declared.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| **The repair left the mistake in place**                                        | `4f61536b6` did not repoint the tiers at `prod`. It made `appdev` production-shaped — added the `/status` healthCheck, `start: npm start` — and wrote the reason into the file: _"The group's stage and production services deploy with zeropsSetup: appdev, so this block must start the real app."_ Correct, and it means the group repo still names a dev setup on two production tiers.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

Read as the owner through the product's own path: a `gitea-signin` throwaway minted with
`NO_ACCESS`, exchanged at the broker for the person's Gitea token, deleted after the call.

**How the first pass got it wrong, twice over.** It named the author from one grep of the wrong
file, and it never read `primer.md` §7.22 — which had already recorded the same artefact on Kai's
`todo/group` and placed it in zcp's recipe composition, waiting on the owner. A fact the ledger
already carried in a neighbouring document was re-derived from scratch, and derived wrong.
Yesterday's rule was **run the probe in the process under test**; this is its twin: **before
attributing a fault, read what the primer already says about it.**

## The half-made Mate: one abort budget for two writes — 2026-09-21

`Lighthouse - Enzo` came up on 2026-09-20 with `core` and no `zcp`, on an ordinary creation moments
after another. Chased a day later from the platform's own process list, which settles it: the
container was never asked for.

| Measured                                         | Value                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `POST /process/search` on the project, ascending | `project.create` FINISHED at 15:55:08, and then **nothing at all** until 16:02 — the next rows are the `stack.create`/`stack.build`/`stack.enableSubdomainAccess` of the recovery a person ran at 16:00:47. No failed process, no error: the platform was never sent the container. |
| What a creation normally costs                   | `zcp` is created 1–4 s after `core`: Ada 14:22:59 → 14:23:00, Uma 15:48:33 → 15:48:34, Wren 15:48:15 → 15:48:19. Enzo: 15:55:07 → 16:00:47, five minutes and forty seconds, all of it the person's.                                                                                 |

The cause is in the client, and reading it needs both halves at once:

- `restAdapter.executeApi` stages **one** `AbortController` per command, with a single timer of
  `min(policy.httpDeadlineMs, remaining)` — `httpDeadlineMs` is 15 s.
- `createProjectWithZeropsMate` makes **two** writes and passed that one signal to both. A project
  POST that spends the budget takes the container with it, and the container request never leaves
  the browser.
- Its `catch` took no argument. An abort that never sent anything and a platform that refused the
  recipe arrived as the same sentence, _"its container setup could not be confirmed"_ — which is
  why this sat unexplained for a day. There was nothing to read.
- The delegation tidy-up after both writes was on the same signal and unguarded, so a cancelled
  tidy-up **failed a creation that had wholly succeeded** — the worst of the three, because the
  person is then told to retry a Mate they already have.

Fixed by one rule: **once the project exists, nothing after it may be skipped or turned into a
failure.** Both the container write and the tidy-up run without the caller's signal, the tidy-up
cannot fail the creation, and the platform's `status`/`code`/`detail` now ride on the error. The
session generation is the one check that still stops the container write, and should: a signed-out
client must send nothing.

Residue worth knowing when reading the test account: `Lighthouse - Enzo` carries `mate:bot:Dara`,
so the left menu calls it Dara. That is the recovery of 16:00:47 naming a Mate afresh, from before
`23d560763` taught it to keep the name — left in place as evidence rather than patched by hand.

projects), driven from a puppeteer browser; the containers installed the pinned mate 0.11.38, so
the server side of the day's commits (`ZeropsMateKey`, the descriptor's `zerops.identity`) was not
in play. Two Mates in a new group `Birth Test`.

| Claim                                                           | Measured                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| --------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| **Hardening runs before admission and restarts nothing**        | `Birth Test - Quinn`, made by _Add Mate_ then _Set up Mate_ (see below): `stack.create`/`stack.build`/`stack.enableSubdomainAccess` 16:49:55, L7 config 16:51:18 (the container answering), the wait's `stack.updateProjectEnvs` 16:51:25 — 7 s after — then the broker's three `stack.updateUserData` 16:52:06. **No `stack.restart` in the project's process list**, on either Mate. `envIsolation=service` on both projects; `ZCP_API_KEY` on the `zcp` service, absent from the project.                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| **The person is not thrown out**                                | Iris held 45 s, Quinn 40 s inside the conversation with no navigation, no `reconnecting`; the hand-off composed in the composer, not sent (D17). The projects card ended with no line ("Iris", "Quinn"), never a stale "Almost there."                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| **The token**                                                   | `zcp-Birth Test - Iris`: `NO_ACCESS`, `BASIC_USER` on its project, 0 delegations (dropped by `createProjectWithMate`). `zcp-Birth Test - Quinn`: `NO_ACCESS`, `BASIC_USER` on itself, `READ_ONLY` on Iris (group reach) — **and 1 delegation left**: the _Set up Mate_ path drops none and `useZeropsGroupReach` dedupes on the group's project-id shape, so it never re-ran when the token appeared. Fix in flight: `hardening` owns the token (lower + drop) as well as the isolation.                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| **A reload mid-birth resumes it**                               | Iris' birth was measured across a page reload: the projects page seeded the wait from the hand-off and the conversation opened 2 s after load.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| **A hidden tab never finishes "Checking your Zerops projects"** | Signed in, then the tab sat behind another for ~25 min: every interest `paused` (reason `background`), the round requires every demanded interest `observing`, `failedInterest` false → "Still checking…" with no cause; visible again, it completed at once. Most likely the 2026-09-20 wedge's cause as well.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| **After the long pause the runtime never re-registered**        | Every interest of both orgs `recovering`, `reason: disconnect`, `attempt: 1`, `completedRegistrations: 0`, `nextRetryAtMs` 10–15 s in the past, for good; receiver epoch +4 per 30 s; each socket to `/web-socket/…` got `SocketSuccess`, sent `ping` at 0 and 15 s, got pongs, and was closed by the server with **1006 at ~18 s**; zero REST registrations in 25 s of fetch instrumentation. The inventory kept a `resolved: []` placeholder for the new ACTIVE project (`resolvedOrCarried` carried the CREATING-time entry), so the wait read "no Zerops Mate container in this project". A page reload recovered. Fix in flight (`runtime.ts` recovery cycle, the placeholder). |
| **A reload between the two creation writes strands the Mate**   | A Vite HMR reload (another agent editing the tree) landed between `POST /client/{id}/project` and the container PUT: `project.create` FINISHED at 16:46:57 and nothing else; the card said "This Mate has no container yet." with _Set up Mate_, which asked for the container at 16:49:55. Same shape as the 2026-09-21 half-made Mate, different cause; the birth reconciler should finish R0 from the hand-off.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| **A write right after a reload is refused**                     | _Set up Mate_ pressed ~8 s after the reload: "Platform write access is not verified. Try again" — the round had not admitted writes yet. The verb should wait for admission or be unpressable until then.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| **The accounts on this laptop**                                 | `z3new1` answers `userNotFound`; `nonmember` signs in but its org `Tst` has no credit — _Create project_ was refused with the platform's words, "Insufficient credit", and nothing was created.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

## Agent sign-in, takeover and sign-out on a live Mate — 2026-09-22/23

Project `111111` (Mate `Uma`), zcp container over the VPN; release 0.11.40, then dev pushes of
`main` (`85c6c372c1`, `34607f6c32`). The owner signed the agents in through the client.

| Claim                                                   | Measured                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **With no agent signed in, 0.11.40 sent the turn**      | The composer showed _Claude Fable 5.1_ selected and Send enabled; the turn started on a logged-out CLI and ended in the thread as "Not logged in · Please run /login" (turn `error`, 2.5 s).                                                                                                                                                          |
| **The Mate's own key writes and deletes service env**   | Key hardened (`GET /user/info` → client role `NO_ACCESS`). `POST /service-stack/{serviceId}/user-data {key, content:"true", sensitive:false}` → 200, process `stack.updateUserData`; the row read back non-sensitive via `GET /service-stack/{serviceId}/env`; `DELETE /user-data/{id}` → 200, row gone. Probe key `ZCP_MATE_PROBE_SIGNOUT`, removed. |
| **Both CLIs log out idempotently**                      | `claude auth logout` (Claude Code 2.1.278) and `codex logout` (codex-cli 0.155.1) exit 0 with no login present.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| **A re-login over a live login shows the same screens** | `claude /login` (cwd `/var/www`) with a valid login: "Select login method" menu, then the authorize URL and "Paste code here if prompted" — as a first login. Esc at the URL step left `.credentials.json` byte-identical and `loggedIn: true`.                                                                                                       |
| **A running turn survives a credential swap**           | Turn started 21:21:01 UTC (a 200 s shell loop); the re-login replaced `.credentials.json` at 21:22:21 (same account); the turn completed 21:24:27 with its answer — the model call after the swap succeeded on the token the process held. Signing out must therefore stop live sessions; a file removal alone leaves them running.                   |
| **The server writes the flag itself**                   | On `34607f6c32` the feed verified both agents after restart and logged `mark-signed-in written … changed: false` for `ZCP_AGENT_OAUTH_CLAUDE_CODE` and `_CODEX`; each is one non-sensitive `true` row. The environment document advertises `agentSignOut: true`.                                                                                      |
| **Sign-out from the agent card**                        | After the owner signed Codex out: `~/.codex/auth.json` absent, `codex login status` → "Not logged in", `ZCP_AGENT_OAUTH_CODEX` gone from the env store; Claude untouched.                                                                                                                                                                             |
| **The dev push needs node-pty's Linux prebuild**        | `mate-dev-push.sh mate` from a Mac failed in `npm install` on the container (node-pty 1.1.0 → node-gyp → "Cannot find module 'node-addon-api'"); fixed in `../zcp` `cc3ba4d6` by bundling the active install's `prebuilds/linux-x64/pty.node`.                                                                                                        |
